===== config/auth.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	return [
     4	
     5	    /*
     6	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
     7	    | Authentication Defaults
     8	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
     9	    |
    10	    | This option controls the default authentication "guard" and password
    11	    | reset options for your application. You may change these defaults
    12	    | as required, but they're a perfect start for most applications.
    13	    |
    14	    */
    15	
    16	    'defaults' => [
    17	        'guard' => 'web',
    18	        'passwords' => 'users',
    19	    ],
    20	
    21	    /*
    22	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    23	    | Authentication Guards
    24	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    25	    |
    26	    | Next, you may define every authentication guard for your application.
    27	    | Of course, a great default configuration has been defined for you
    28	    | here which uses session storage and the Eloquent user provider.
    29	    |
    30	    | All authentication drivers have a user provider. This defines how the
    31	    | users are actually retrieved out of your database or other storage
    32	    | mechanisms used by this application to persist your user's data.
    33	    |
    34	    | Supported: "session"
    35	    |
    36	    */
    37	
    38	    'guards' => [
    39	        'web' => [
    40	            'driver' => 'session',
    41	            'provider' => 'users',
    42	        ],
    43	    ],
    44	
    45	    /*
    46	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47	    | User Providers
    48	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49	    |
    50	    | All authentication drivers have a user provider. This defines how the
    51	    | users are actually retrieved out of your database or other storage
    52	    | mechanisms used by this application to persist your user's data.
    53	    |
    54	    | If you have multiple user tables or models you may configure multiple
    55	    | sources which represent each model / table. These sources may then
    56	    | be assigned to any extra authentication guards you have defined.
    57	    |
    58	    | Supported: "database", "eloquent"
    59	    |
    60	    */
    61	
    62	    'providers' => [
    63	        'users' => [
    64	            'driver' => 'eloquent',
    65	            'model' => App\Models\User::class,
    66	        ],
    67	
    68	        // 'users' => [
    69	        //     'driver' => 'database',
    70	        //     'table' => 'users',
    71	        // ],
    72	    ],
    73	
    74	    /*
    75	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    76	    | Resetting Passwords
    77	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    78	    |
    79	    | You may specify multiple password reset configurations if you have more
    80	    | than one user table or model in the application and you want to have
    81	    | separate password reset settings based on the specific user types.
    82	    |
    83	    | The expiry time is the number of minutes that each reset token will be
    84	    | considered valid. This security feature keeps tokens short-lived so
    85	    | they have less time to be guessed. You may change this as needed.
    86	    |
    87	    | The throttle setting is the number of seconds a user must wait before
    88	    | generating more password reset tokens. This prevents the user from
    89	    | quickly generating a very large amount of password reset tokens.
    90	    |
    91	    */
    92	
    93	    'passwords' => [
    94	        'users' => [
    95	            'provider' => 'users',
    96	            'table' => 'password_reset_tokens',
    97	            'expire' => 60,
    98	            'throttle' => 60,
    99	        ],
   100	    ],
   101	
   102	    /*
   103	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
   104	    | Password Confirmation Timeout
   105	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
   106	    |
   107	    | Here you may define the amount of seconds before a password confirmation
   108	    | times out and the user is prompted to re-enter their password via the
   109	    | confirmation screen. By default, the timeout lasts for three hours.
   110	    |
   111	    */
   112	
   113	    'password_timeout' => 10800,
   114	
   115	];

    14	    | guards that is already present in your "auth" configuration file.
    15	    |
    16	    */
    17

    19

    22	    | Fortify Password Broker
    23	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    24	    |

    26	    | is resetting their password. This configured value should match one
    27	    | of your password brokers setup in your "auth" configuration file.
    28	    |
    29	    */

    32

    40	    | address of the users but you are free to change this value here.
    41	    |
    42	    | Out of the box, Fortify expects forgot password and reset password
    43	    | requests to have a field named 'email'. If the application uses
    44	    | another name for the field you may define it below as needed.
    45	    |
    46	    */
    47

    21	        Sanctum::currentApplicationUrlWithPort()
    22	    ))),

    30	    | Sanctum is trying to authenticate a request. If none of these guards
    31	    | are able to authenticate the request, Sanctum will use the bearer
    32	    | token that's present on an incoming request for authentication.
    33	    |
    34	    */
    35

    37	
    38	    /*

    48

    50

    57	    | security scanning initiatives maintained by open source platforms
    58	    | that notify developers if they commit tokens into repositories.
    59	    |

    63

     7	    /*

    15	    |

    20	
    21	    'driver' => env('SESSION_DRIVER', 'file'),
    22	
    23	    /*
    24	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    25	    | Session Lifetime
    26	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27	    |
    28	    | Here you may specify the number of minutes that you wish the session

    30	    | to immediately expire on the browser closing, set that option.
    31	    |
    32	    */
    33	
    34	    'lifetime' => env('SESSION_LIFETIME', 120),
    35	
    36	    'expire_on_close' => false,
    37

    39	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    40	    | Session Encryption
    41	    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    42	    |

    48

    61

    86	    */
    87

===== database/factories/BookFactory.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Database\Factories;
     4	
     5	use App\Models\User;
     6	use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\Factory;
     7	
     8	/**
     9	 * @extends \Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\Factory<\App\Models\Book>
    10	 */
    11	class BookFactory extends Factory
    12	{
    13	    /**
    14	     * Define the model's default state.
    15	     *
    16	     * @return array<string, mixed>
    17	     */
    18	    public function definition(): array
    19	    {
    20	        return [
    21	            'title' => fake()->sentence(3),
    22	            'author' => fake()->name(),
    23	            'isbn' => fake()->unique()->numerify('978##########'),
    24	            'published_date' => fake()->date('Y-m-d'),
    25	            'description' => fake()->paragraph(),
    26	            'image_url' => 'https://placehold.co/200x300',
    27	            'user_id' => User::factory(),
    28	        ];
    29	    }
    30	}

===== database/factories/UserFactory.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Database\Factories;
     4	
     5	use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\Factory;
     6	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
     7	use Illuminate\Support\Str;
     8	
     9	/**
    10	 * @extends \Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\Factory<\App\Models\User>
    11	 */
    12	class UserFactory extends Factory
    13	{
    14	    /**
    15	     * The current password being used by the factory.
    16	     */
    17	    protected static ?string $password;
    18	
    19	    /**
    20	     * Define the model's default state.
    21	     *
    22	     * @return array<string, mixed>
    23	     */
    24	    public function definition(): array
    25	    {
    26	        return [
    27	            'name' => fake()->name(),
    28	            'email' => fake()->unique()->safeEmail(),
    29	            'email_verified_at' => now(),
    30	            'password' => static::$password ??= Hash::make('password'),
    31	            'remember_token' => Str::random(10),
    32	        ];
    33	    }
    34	
    35	    /**
    36	     * Indicate that the model's email address should be unverified.
    37	     */
    38	    public function unverified(): static
    39	    {
    40	        return $this->state(fn (array $attributes) => [
    41	            'email_verified_at' => null,
    42	        ]);
    43	    }
    44	}

===== database/seeders/FavoriteSeeder.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Database\Seeders;
     4	
     5	use App\Models\Book;
     6	use App\Models\User;
     7	use Illuminate\Database\Seeder;
     8	
     9	class FavoriteSeeder extends Seeder
    10	{
    11	    /**
    12	     * Run the database seeds.
    13	     */
    14	    public function run(): void
    15	    {
    16	        $users = User::orderBy('id')->get();
    17	        $bookIds = Book::orderBy('id')->pluck('id')->all();
    18	        $total = count($bookIds);
    19	
    20	        // 各ユーザーに3〜5冊のお気に入りを決定的に割り当てる
    21	        $sizes = [4, 3, 5, 3, 4];
    22	
    23	        foreach ($users as $index => $user) {
    24	            $size = $sizes[$index % count($sizes)];
    25	            $favorites = [];
    26	            for ($j = 0; $j < $size; $j++) {
    27	                $favorites[] = $bookIds[($index * 2 + $j) % $total];
    28	            }
    29	
    30	            $user->favoriteBooks()->syncWithoutDetaching($favorites);
    31	        }
    32	    }
    33	}

===== database/seeders/ReadingPlanSeeder.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Database\Seeders;
     4	
     5	use App\Enums\ReadingPlanStatus;
     6	use App\Models\Book;
     7	use App\Models\ReadingPlan;
     8	use App\Models\User;
     9	use Illuminate\Database\Seeder;
    10	use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;
    11	
    12	class ReadingPlanSeeder extends Seeder
    13	{
    14	    /**
    15	     * Run the database seeds.
    16	     *
    17	     * 採点者がいつ実行しても同じ挙動になるよう、target_date は Carbon::today() 起点で動的に設定する。
    18	     */
    19	    public function run(): void
    20	    {
    21	        // 山田太郎（主要シナリオ5件）と鈴木花子（他ユーザー認可テスト用1件）
    22	        $yamada = User::where('email', 'yamada@example.com')->first();
    23	        $suzuki = User::where('email', 'suzuki@example.com')->first();
    24	
    25	        // 計画ごとに異なる書籍を割り当てる（進行中の重複制御に抵触しないようにする）
    26	        $books = Book::orderBy('id')->take(6)->get();
    27	
    28	        $plans = [
    29	            // 1. 3日前リマインダー対象
    30	            [
    31	                'user_id' => $yamada->id,
    32	                'book_id' => $books[0]->id,
    33	                'target_date' => Carbon::today()->addDays(3),
    34	                'status' => ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress,
    35	                'completed_at' => null,
    36	            ],
    37	            // 2. 当日リマインダー対象
    38	            [
    39	                'user_id' => $yamada->id,
    40	                'book_id' => $books[1]->id,
    41	                'target_date' => Carbon::today(),
    42	                'status' => ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress,
    43	                'completed_at' => null,
    44	            ],
    45	            // 3. 本バッチで expired へ変わる（3日前）
    46	            [
    47	                'user_id' => $yamada->id,
    48	                'book_id' => $books[2]->id,
    49	                'target_date' => Carbon::today()->subDays(3),
    50	                'status' => ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress,
    51	                'completed_at' => null,
    52	            ],
    53	            // 4. リマインダー対象外（7日後）
    54	            [
    55	                'user_id' => $yamada->id,
    56	                'book_id' => $books[3]->id,
    57	                'target_date' => Carbon::today()->addDays(7),
    58	                'status' => ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress,
    59	                'completed_at' => null,
    60	            ],
    61	            // 5. 完了済み
    62	            [
    63	                'user_id' => $yamada->id,
    64	                'book_id' => $books[4]->id,
    65	                'target_date' => Carbon::today()->subDays(10),
    66	                'status' => ReadingPlanStatus::Completed,
    67	                'completed_at' => Carbon::today()->subDays(5),
    68	            ],
    69	            // 6. 鈴木花子（山田ログイン中の 403 確認用）
    70	            [
    71	                'user_id' => $suzuki->id,
    72	                'book_id' => $books[5]->id,
    73	                'target_date' => Carbon::today()->addDays(5),
    74	                'status' => ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress,
    75	                'completed_at' => null,
    76	            ],
    77	        ];
    78	
    79	        foreach ($plans as $plan) {
    80	            ReadingPlan::create($plan);
    81	        }
    82	    }
    83	}

===== database/seeders/ReviewLikeSeeder.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Database\Seeders;
     4	
     5	use App\Models\Review;
     6	use App\Models\User;
     7	use Illuminate\Database\Seeder;
     8	
     9	class ReviewLikeSeeder extends Seeder
    10	{
    11	    /**
    12	     * Run the database seeds.
    13	     */
    14	    public function run(): void
    15	    {
    16	        $users = User::orderBy('id')->get();
    17	        $reviews = Review::orderBy('id')->get();
    18	        $userCount = $users->count();
    19	
    20	        foreach ($reviews as $reviewIndex => $review) {
    21	            // 各レビューに0〜3人がいいね（投稿者本人は除く）
    22	            $likeCount = $reviewIndex % 4;
    23	            $likers = [];
    24	
    25	            for ($j = 0; count($likers) < $likeCount && $j < $userCount; $j++) {
    26	                $user = $users[($reviewIndex + $j) % $userCount];
    27	                if ($user->id !== $review->user_id && ! in_array($user->id, $likers)) {
    28	                    $likers[] = $user->id;
    29	                }
    30	            }
    31	
    32	            if (! empty($likers)) {
    33	                $review->likedByUsers()->syncWithoutDetaching($likers);
    34	            }
    35	        }
    36	    }
    37	}

===== database/seeders/ReviewSeeder.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Database\Seeders;
     4	
     5	use App\Models\Book;
     6	use App\Models\Review;
     7	use App\Models\User;
     8	use Illuminate\Database\Seeder;
     9	
    10	class ReviewSeeder extends Seeder
    11	{
    12	    /**
    13	     * Run the database seeds.
    14	     */
    15	    public function run(): void
    16	    {
    17	        $users = User::orderBy('id')->get();
    18	        $books = Book::orderBy('id')->get();
    19	
    20	        // 評価別の日本語コメントテンプレート（5段階）
    21	        $commentTemplates = [
    22	            5 => ['素晴らしい本でした！', '人生が変わりました。', '何度も読み返しています。'],
    23	            4 => ['とても参考になりました。', '読みやすくておすすめです。', '期待通りの内容でした。'],
    24	            3 => ['普通でした。', '可もなく不可もなく。', '期待したほどではなかった。'],
    25	            2 => ['少し期待外れでした。', '内容が薄い印象。', 'もう少し深掘りしてほしかった。'],
    26	            1 => ['残念ながら合いませんでした。', '期待と違いました。'],
    27	        ];
    28	
    29	        foreach ($books as $book) {
    30	            $reviewCount = rand(2, 4);
    31	
    32	            foreach ($users->random($reviewCount) as $user) {
    33	                $rating = rand(1, 5);
    34	                $comments = $commentTemplates[$rating];
    35	
    36	                Review::create([
    37	                    'user_id' => $user->id,
    38	                    'book_id' => $book->id,
    39	                    'rating' => $rating,
    40	                    'comment' => $comments[array_rand($comments)],
    41	                ]);
    42	            }
    43	        }
    44	    }
    45	}

===== public/.htaccess
     1	<IfModule mod_rewrite.c>
     2	    <IfModule mod_negotiation.c>
     3	        Options -MultiViews -Indexes
     4	    </IfModule>
     5	
     6	    RewriteEngine On
     7	
     8	    # Handle Authorization Header
     9	    RewriteCond %{HTTP:Authorization} .
    10	    RewriteRule .* - [E=HTTP_AUTHORIZATION:%{HTTP:Authorization}]
    11	
    12	    # Redirect Trailing Slashes If Not A Folder...
    13	    RewriteCond %{REQUEST_FILENAME} !-d
    14	    RewriteCond %{REQUEST_URI} (.+)/$
    15	    RewriteRule ^ %1 [L,R=301]
    16	
    17	    # Send Requests To Front Controller...
    18	    RewriteCond %{REQUEST_FILENAME} !-d
    19	    RewriteCond %{REQUEST_FILENAME} !-f
    20	    RewriteRule ^ index.php [L]
    21	</IfModule>

===== public/index.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	use Illuminate\Contracts\Http\Kernel;
     4	use Illuminate\Http\Request;
     5	
     6	define('LARAVEL_START', microtime(true));
     7	
     8	/*
     9	|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    10	| Check If The Application Is Under Maintenance
    11	|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12	|
    13	| If the application is in maintenance / demo mode via the "down" command
    14	| we will load this file so that any pre-rendered content can be shown
    15	| instead of starting the framework, which could cause an exception.
    16	|
    17	*/
    18	
    19	if (file_exists($maintenance = __DIR__.'/../storage/framework/maintenance.php')) {
    20	    require $maintenance;
    21	}
    22	
    23	/*
    24	|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    25	| Register The Auto Loader
    26	|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27	|
    28	| Composer provides a convenient, automatically generated class loader for
    29	| this application. We just need to utilize it! We'll simply require it
    30	| into the script here so we don't need to manually load our classes.
    31	|
    32	*/
    33	
    34	require __DIR__.'/../vendor/autoload.php';
    35	
    36	/*
    37	|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    38	| Run The Application
    39	|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    40	|
    41	| Once we have the application, we can handle the incoming request using
    42	| the application's HTTP kernel. Then, we will send the response back
    43	| to this client's browser, allowing them to enjoy our application.
    44	|
    45	*/
    46	
    47	$app = require_once __DIR__.'/../bootstrap/app.php';
    48	
    49	$kernel = $app->make(Kernel::class);
    50	
    51	$response = $kernel->handle(
    52	    $request = Request::capture()
    53	)->send();
    54	
    55	$kernel->terminate($request, $response);

===== resources/views/books/create.blade.php
     1	<x-app-layout>
     2	    <x-slot name="header">
     3	        <h2 class="font-semibold text-xl text-gray-800 leading-tight">
     4	            {{ __('書籍の登録') }}
     5	        </h2>
     6	    </x-slot>
     7	
     8	    <div class="py-12">
     9	        <div class="max-w-2xl mx-auto sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
    10	            <div class="bg-white overflow-hidden shadow-sm sm:rounded-lg">
    11	                <div class="p-6 text-gray-900">
    12	                    <div class="bg-indigo-50 border border-indigo-100 rounded-lg p-4 mb-6">
    13	                        <h3 class="font-medium text-gray-800 mb-1">📖 ISBN から書籍情報を自動入力</h3>
    14	                        <p class="text-sm text-gray-600 mb-3">13桁の ISBN を入力すると、Google Books API から書籍情報を取得してフォームを自動補完します。</p>
    15	                        <div class="flex gap-2">
    16	                            <input type="text" id="isbn_search" value=""
    17	                                class="border-gray-300 focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-indigo-500 rounded-md shadow-sm block w-full"
    18	                                placeholder="例: 9784101010014">
    19	                            <button type="button" id="isbn_search_button"
    20	                                class="shrink-0 bg-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white font-bold py-2 px-4 rounded whitespace-nowrap">
    21	                                🔍 検索
    22	                            </button>
    23	                        </div>
    24	                        <p id="isbn_search_message" class="text-sm mt-2 hidden"></p>
    25	                    </div>
    26	
    27	                    <form action="{{ route('books.store') }}" method="POST" novalidate>
    28	                        @include('books._form')
    29	
    30	                        <div class="flex items-center justify-end mt-6 pt-6 border-t border-gray-200">
    31	                            <a href="{{ route('books.index') }}" class="text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-900 mr-4">
    32	                                キャンセル
    33	                            </a>
    34	                            <button type="submit" class="bg-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white font-bold py-2 px-6 rounded">
    35	                                登録
    36	                            </button>
    37	                        </div>
    38	                    </form>
    39	                </div>
    40	            </div>
    41	        </div>
    42	    </div>
    43	
    44	    @push('scripts')
    45	        <script>
    46	            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
    47	                const button = document.getElementById('isbn_search_button');
    48	                const input = document.getElementById('isbn_search');
    49	                const message = document.getElementById('isbn_search_message');
    50	
    51	                const showMessage = function (text, isError) {
    52	                    message.textContent = text;
    53	                    message.classList.remove('hidden', 'text-red-600', 'text-green-600');
    54	                    message.classList.add(isError ? 'text-red-600' : 'text-green-600');
    55	                };
    56	
    57	                const setValue = function (id, value) {
    58	                    const field = document.getElementById(id);
    59	                    if (field && value) {
    60	                        field.value = value;
    61	                    }
    62	                };
    63	
    64	                button.addEventListener('click', async function () {
    65	                    const isbn = input.value.trim();
    66	                    message.classList.add('hidden');
    67	
    68	                    try {
    69	                        const response = await fetch(`/books/isbn/${encodeURIComponent(isbn)}`, {
    70	                            headers: { 'Accept': 'application/json' },
    71	                        });
    72	                        const data = await response.json();
    73	
    74	                        if (!response.ok) {
    75	                            showMessage(data.message ?? '書籍情報の取得に失敗しました', true);
    76	                            return;
    77	                        }
    78	
    79	                        setValue('title', data.title);
    80	                        setValue('author', data.author);
    81	                        setValue('description', data.description);
    82	                        setValue('image_url', data.image_url);
    83	                        setValue('published_date', data.published_date);
    84	                        document.getElementById('isbn').value = isbn;
    85	
    86	                        if (data.published_date_padded) {
    87	                            showMessage('出版日は年月までの情報のため、日付は仮の値（1日）を自動設定しました。正しい日付が分かる場合は修正してください。', false);
    88	                        } else {
    89	                            showMessage('書籍情報を自動入力しました', false);
    90	                        }
    91	                    } catch (e) {
    92	                        showMessage('書籍情報の取得に失敗しました', true);
    93	                    }
    94	                });
    95	            });
    96	        </script>
    97	    @endpush
    98	</x-app-layout>

===== resources/views/components/modal.blade.php
     1	@props([
     2	    'name',
     3	    'show' => false,
     4	    'maxWidth' => '2xl'
     5	])
     6	
     7	@php
     8	$maxWidth = [
     9	    'sm' => 'sm:max-w-sm',
    10	    'md' => 'sm:max-w-md',
    11	    'lg' => 'sm:max-w-lg',
    12	    'xl' => 'sm:max-w-xl',
    13	    '2xl' => 'sm:max-w-2xl',
    14	][$maxWidth];
    15	@endphp
    16	
    17	<div
    18	    x-data="{
    19	        show: @js($show),
    20	        focusables() {
    21	            // All focusable element types...
    22	            let selector = 'a, button, input:not([type=\'hidden\']), textarea, select, details, [tabindex]:not([tabindex=\'-1\'])'
    23	            return [...$el.querySelectorAll(selector)]
    24	                // All non-disabled elements...
    25	                .filter(el => ! el.hasAttribute('disabled'))
    26	        },
    27	        firstFocusable() { return this.focusables()[0] },
    28	        lastFocusable() { return this.focusables().slice(-1)[0] },
    29	        nextFocusable() { return this.focusables()[this.nextFocusableIndex()] || this.firstFocusable() },
    30	        prevFocusable() { return this.focusables()[this.prevFocusableIndex()] || this.lastFocusable() },
    31	        nextFocusableIndex() { return (this.focusables().indexOf(document.activeElement) + 1) % (this.focusables().length + 1) },
    32	        prevFocusableIndex() { return Math.max(0, this.focusables().indexOf(document.activeElement)) -1 },
    33	    }"
    34	    x-init="$watch('show', value => {
    35	        if (value) {
    36	            document.body.classList.add('overflow-y-hidden');
    37	            {{ $attributes->has('focusable') ? 'setTimeout(() => firstFocusable().focus(), 100)' : '' }}
    38	        } else {
    39	            document.body.classList.remove('overflow-y-hidden');
    40	        }
    41	    })"
    42	    x-on:open-modal.window="$event.detail == '{{ $name }}' ? show = true : null"
    43	    x-on:close-modal.window="$event.detail == '{{ $name }}' ? show = false : null"
    44	    x-on:close.stop="show = false"
    45	    x-on:keydown.escape.window="show = false"
    46	    x-on:keydown.tab.prevent="$event.shiftKey || nextFocusable().focus()"
    47	    x-on:keydown.shift.tab.prevent="prevFocusable().focus()"
    48	    x-show="show"
    49	    class="fixed inset-0 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-6 sm:px-0 z-50"
    50	    style="display: {{ $show ? 'block' : 'none' }};"
    51	>
    52	    <div
    53	        x-show="show"
    54	        class="fixed inset-0 transform transition-all"
    55	        x-on:click="show = false"
    56	        x-transition:enter="ease-out duration-300"
    57	        x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0"
    58	        x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100"
    59	        x-transition:leave="ease-in duration-200"
    60	        x-transition:leave-start="opacity-100"
    61	        x-transition:leave-end="opacity-0"
    62	    >
    63	        <div class="absolute inset-0 bg-gray-500 opacity-75"></div>
    64	    </div>
    65	
    66	    <div
    67	        x-show="show"
    68	        class="mb-6 bg-white rounded-lg overflow-hidden shadow-xl transform transition-all sm:w-full {{ $maxWidth }} sm:mx-auto"
    69	        x-transition:enter="ease-out duration-300"
    70	        x-transition:enter-start="opacity-0 translate-y-4 sm:translate-y-0 sm:scale-95"
    71	        x-transition:enter-end="opacity-100 translate-y-0 sm:scale-100"
    72	        x-transition:leave="ease-in duration-200"
    73	        x-transition:leave-start="opacity-100 translate-y-0 sm:scale-100"
    74	        x-transition:leave-end="opacity-0 translate-y-4 sm:translate-y-0 sm:scale-95"
    75	    >
    76	        {{ $slot }}
    77	    </div>
    78	</div>

     1	<x-app-layout>
     2	    <x-slot name="header">
     3	        <h2 class="font-semibold text-xl text-gray-800 leading-tight">
     4	            通知一覧
     5	        </h2>
     6	    </x-slot>
     7	
     8	    <div class="py-12">
     9	        <div class="max-w-7xl mx-auto sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
    10	            @if(session('success'))
    11	                <div class="bg-green-100 border border-green-400 text-green-700 px-4 py-3 rounded mb-4">
    12	                    {{ session('success') }}
    13	                </div>
    14	            @endif
    15	
    16	            <div class="bg-white shadow-sm sm:rounded-lg overflow-hidden">
    17	                @if($notifications->isEmpty())
    18	                    <div class="px-6 py-16 text-center">
    19	                        <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="mx-auto h-12 w-12 text-gray-300" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
    20	                            <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="1.5" d="M15 17h5l-1.405-1.405A2.032 2.032 0 0118 14.158V11a6.002 6.002 0 00-4-5.659V5a2 2 0 10-4 0v.341C7.67 6.165 6 8.388 6 11v3.159c0 .538-.214 1.055-.595 1.436L4 17h5m6 0v1a3 3 0 11-6 0v-1m6 0H9" />
    21	                        </svg>
    22	                        <p class="mt-3 text-sm text-gray-500">通知はありません。</p>
    23	                    </div>
    24	                @else
    25	                    <ul class="divide-y divide-gray-100">
    26	                        @foreach($notifications as $notification)
    27	                            @php
    28	                                $timing = $notification->data['timing'] ?? null;
    29	                                $isUnread = $notification->read_at === null;
    30	
    31	                                // タイミング別の色・アイコン定義（Tailwind 静的解析のため class 全体をリテラルで書く）
    32	                                $style = match ($timing) {
    33	                                    'three_days_before' => [
    34	                                        'border' => 'bg-blue-500',
    35	                                        'iconBg' => $isUnread ? 'bg-blue-100' : 'bg-gray-100',
    36	                                        'iconColor' => $isUnread ? 'text-blue-600' : 'text-gray-400',
    37	                                        'icon' => 'calendar',
    38	                                    ],
    39	                                    'on_due_date' => [
    40	                                        'border' => 'bg-yellow-500',
    41	                                        'iconBg' => $isUnread ? 'bg-yellow-100' : 'bg-gray-100',
    42	                                        'iconColor' => $isUnread ? 'text-yellow-700' : 'text-gray-400',
    43	                                        'icon' => 'clock',
    44	                                    ],
    45	                                    'three_days_after' => [
    46	                                        'border' => 'bg-red-500',
    47	                                        'iconBg' => $isUnread ? 'bg-red-100' : 'bg-gray-100',
    48	                                        'iconColor' => $isUnread ? 'text-red-600' : 'text-gray-400',
    49	                                        'icon' => 'warning',
    50	                                    ],
    51	                                    default => [
    52	                                        'border' => 'bg-gray-300',
    53	                                        'iconBg' => 'bg-gray-100',
    54	                                        'iconColor' => 'text-gray-400',
    55	                                        'icon' => 'bell',
    56	                                    ],
    57	                                };
    58	                            @endphp
    59	                            <li class="relative {{ $isUnread ? 'bg-blue-50/40' : 'bg-white' }} hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors">
    60	                                {{-- Left border for unread --}}
    61	                                @if($isUnread)
    62	                                    <span class="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 w-1 {{ $style['border'] }}" aria-hidden="true"></span>
    63	                                @endif
    64	
    65	                                <div class="px-6 py-4 flex items-start">
    66	                                    {{-- Icon --}}
    67	                                    <div class="flex-shrink-0">
    68	                                        <span class="inline-flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full {{ $style['iconBg'] }}">
    69	                                            @if($style['icon'] === 'calendar')
    70	                                                <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="h-5 w-5 {{ $style['iconColor'] }}" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
    71	                                                    <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M8 7V3m8 4V3m-9 8h10M5 21h14a2 2 0 002-2V7a2 2 0 00-2-2H5a2 2 0 00-2 2v12a2 2 0 002 2z" />
    72	                                                </svg>
    73	                                            @elseif($style['icon'] === 'clock')
    74	                                                <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="h-5 w-5 {{ $style['iconColor'] }}" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
    75	                                                    <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 8v4l3 3m6-3a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z" />
    76	                                                </svg>
    77	                                            @elseif($style['icon'] === 'warning')
    78	                                                <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="h-5 w-5 {{ $style['iconColor'] }}" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">
    79	                                                    <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 9v2m0 4h.01m-6.938 4h13.856c1.54 0 2.502-1.667 1.732-3L13.732 4c-.77-1.333-2.694-1.333-3.464 0L3.34 16c-.77 1.333.192 3 1.732 3z" />
    80	                                                </svg>

===== routes/api.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	use App\Http\Controllers\Api\V1\BookController;
     4	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;
     5	
     6	/*
     7	|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
     8	| API Routes
     9	|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
    10	|
    11	| Here is where you can register API routes for your application. These
    12	| routes are loaded by the RouteServiceProvider and all of them will
    13	| be assigned to the "api" middleware group. Make something great!
    14	|
    15	*/
    16	
    17	Route::prefix('v1')->group(function () {
    18	    // 読み取り系（認証なし・公開）
    19	    Route::get('/books', [BookController::class, 'index']);
    20	    Route::get('/books/{book}', [BookController::class, 'show']);
    21	
    22	    // 書き込み系（Sanctumトークン認証必須）
    23	    Route::middleware('auth:sanctum')->group(function () {
    24	        Route::post('/books', [BookController::class, 'store']);
    25	        Route::put('/books/{book}', [BookController::class, 'update']);
    26	        Route::delete('/books/{book}', [BookController::class, 'destroy']);
    27	    });
    28	});

===== setup.sh
     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	set -euo pipefail
     3	
     4	[ -f .env ] || cp .env.example .env
     5	
     6	docker run --rm \
     7	    -u "$(id -u):$(id -g)" \
     8	    -v "$(pwd):/var/www/html" \
     9	    -w /var/www/html \
    10	    -e COMPOSER_CACHE_DIR=/tmp/composer_cache \
    11	    laravelsail/php82-composer:latest \
    12	    composer install
    13	
    14	./vendor/bin/sail up -d
    15	
    16	grep -q '^APP_KEY=base64:' .env || ./vendor/bin/sail artisan key:generate
    17	
    18	echo "waiting for mysql..."
    19	until ./vendor/bin/sail artisan migrate --force >/dev/null 2>&1; do sleep 3; done
    20	
    21	echo "app: http://localhost:$(grep '^APP_PORT=' .env | cut -d= -f2)"
    22	echo "pma: http://localhost:8080"

     1	<?php

     8	trait CreatesApplication
     9	{
    10	    /**
    11	     * Creates the application.
    12	     */

    14	    {
    15	        $app = require __DIR__.'/../bootstrap/app.php';

===== tests/Feature/Api/BookApiTest.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Tests\Feature\Api;
     4	
     5	use App\Models\Book;
     6	use App\Models\Genre;
     7	use App\Models\Review;
     8	use App\Models\User;
     9	use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
    10	use Laravel\Sanctum\Sanctum;
    11	use Tests\TestCase;
    12	
    13	/**
    14	 * 走行⑩：公開API（Sanctumトークン認証）。検品表 G ＋ 発注書§7の書き直し対象。
    15	 * 読み取り系（GET 2本）は認証不要。書き込み系（POST/PUT/DELETE 3本）は auth:sanctum 必須。
    16	 */
    17	class BookApiTest extends TestCase
    18	{
    19	    use RefreshDatabase;
    20	
    21	    /**
    22	     * ジャンル付き書籍を作成するヘルパ。
    23	     */
    24	    private function makeBook(array $attrs = []): Book
    25	    {
    26	        $book = Book::factory()->create($attrs);
    27	        $book->genres()->sync([Genre::factory()->create()->id]);
    28	
    29	        return $book;
    30	    }
    31	
    32	    /**
    33	     * 有効な書き込みペイロード（API側はisbn/published_date必須のまま）。
    34	     *
    35	     * @return array<string, mixed>
    36	     */
    37	    private function validPayload(array $overrides = []): array
    38	    {
    39	        return array_merge([
    40	            'title' => 'API書籍',
    41	            'author' => 'API著者',
    42	            'isbn' => '9784111111119',
    43	            'published_date' => '2021-05-05',
    44	            'description' => 'desc',
    45	            'image_url' => 'https://example.com/x.jpg',
    46	            'genres' => [Genre::factory()->create()->id],
    47	        ], $overrides);
    48	    }
    49	
    50	    // ================= G-4: 読み取り系（GET 2本）は認証なしで200 =================
    51	
    52	    /** G-4: GET一覧は未認証で200 */
    53	    public function test_index_is_public_returns_200(): void
    54	    {
    55	        Book::factory()->count(3)->create()->each(
    56	            fn ($b) => $b->genres()->sync([Genre::factory()->create()->id])
    57	        );
    58	
    59	        $this->getJson('/api/v1/books')
    60	            ->assertOk()
    61	            ->assertJsonPath('meta.total', 3);
    62	    }
    63	
    64	    /** G-4: GET詳細は未認証で200 */
    65	    public function test_show_is_public_returns_200(): void
    66	    {
    67	        $book = $this->makeBook();
    68	        Review::factory()->create(['book_id' => $book->id, 'rating' => 4]);
    69	
    70	        $this->getJson("/api/v1/books/{$book->id}")
    71	            ->assertOk()
    72	            ->assertJsonPath('data.id', $book->id);
    73	    }
    74	
    75	    /** 一覧：keyword絞り込みで該当書籍のみ返る（BookController::index の keyword 分岐） */
    76	    public function test_index_keyword_filter(): void
    77	    {
    78	        $hit = Book::factory()->create(['title' => 'APIキーワードヒット本', 'author' => '著者X']);
    79	        $hit->genres()->sync([Genre::factory()->create()->id]);
    80	        $miss = Book::factory()->create(['title' => '無関係の本', 'author' => '著者Y']);
    81	        $miss->genres()->sync([Genre::factory()->create()->id]);
    82	
    83	        $this->getJson('/api/v1/books?keyword=キーワードヒット')
    84	            ->assertOk()
    85	            ->assertJsonPath('meta.total', 1)
    86	            ->assertJsonFragment(['id' => $hit->id])
    87	            ->assertJsonMissing(['id' => $miss->id]);
    88	    }
    89	
    90	    /** 一覧：genre_id絞り込みで該当書籍のみ返る（BookController::index の genre_id 分岐） */
    91	    public function test_index_genre_filter(): void
    92	    {
    93	        $genre = Genre::factory()->create();
    94	        $otherGenre = Genre::factory()->create();
    95	
    96	        $inGenre = Book::factory()->create(['title' => '対象ジャンルの本']);
    97	        $inGenre->genres()->sync([$genre->id]);
    98	        $outGenre = Book::factory()->create(['title' => '別ジャンルの本']);
    99	        $outGenre->genres()->sync([$otherGenre->id]);
   100	
   101	        $this->getJson('/api/v1/books?genre_id='.$genre->id)
   102	            ->assertOk()
   103	            ->assertJsonPath('meta.total', 1)
   104	            ->assertJsonFragment(['id' => $inGenre->id])
   105	            ->assertJsonMissing(['id' => $outGenre->id]);
   106	    }
   107	
   108	    // ================= G-1: 書き込み系の正常系（Sanctum認証） =================
   109	
   110	    /** G-1: POST（登録）はSanctum認証で201・レコード作成 */
   111	    public function test_store_success_with_sanctum(): void
   112	    {
   113	        $user = User::factory()->create();
   114	        Sanctum::actingAs($user);
   115	
   116	        $this->postJson('/api/v1/books', $this->validPayload(['isbn' => '9784111111119']))
   117	            ->assertStatus(201)
   118	            ->assertJsonPath('data.title', 'API書籍');
   119	
   120	        $this->assertDatabaseHas('books', ['isbn' => '9784111111119', 'user_id' => $user->id]);
   121	    }
   122	
   123	    /** G-1: PUT（更新）はSanctum認証（所有者）で200・レコード更新 */
   124	    public function test_update_success_with_sanctum(): void
   125	    {
   126	        $user = User::factory()->create();
   127	        $book = $this->makeBook(['isbn' => '9784222222227', 'user_id' => $user->id]);
   128	        Sanctum::actingAs($user);
   129	
   130	        $this->putJson("/api/v1/books/{$book->id}", $this->validPayload([
   131	            'title' => '更新後タイトル',
   132	            'isbn' => '9784222222227',
   133	        ]))
   134	            ->assertOk()
   135	            ->assertJsonPath('data.title', '更新後タイトル');
   136	
   137	        $this->assertDatabaseHas('books', ['id' => $book->id, 'title' => '更新後タイトル']);
   138	    }
   139	
   140	    /** G-1: DELETE（削除）はSanctum認証（所有者）で204・論理削除 */
   141	    public function test_destroy_success_with_sanctum(): void
   142	    {
   143	        $user = User::factory()->create();
   144	        $book = $this->makeBook(['user_id' => $user->id]);
   145	        Sanctum::actingAs($user);
   146	
   147	        $this->deleteJson("/api/v1/books/{$book->id}")->assertStatus(204);
   148	        $this->assertSoftDeleted('books', ['id' => $book->id]);
   149	    }
   150	
   151	    // ================= G-2: 未認証の書き込みは401（メソッドごと個別） =================
   152	
   153	    /** G-2: 未認証POSTは401「認証が必要です。」 */
   154	    public function test_store_unauthenticated_returns_401(): void
   155	    {
   156	        $this->postJson('/api/v1/books', $this->validPayload())
   157	            ->assertStatus(401)
   158	            ->assertJson(['message' => '認証が必要です。']);
   159	    }
   160	
   161	    /** G-2: 未認証PUTは401「認証が必要です。」 */
   162	    public function test_update_unauthenticated_returns_401(): void
   163	    {
   164	        $book = $this->makeBook();
   165	
   166	        $this->putJson("/api/v1/books/{$book->id}", $this->validPayload())
   167	            ->assertStatus(401)
   168	            ->assertJson(['message' => '認証が必要です。']);
   169	    }
   170	
   171	    /** G-2: 未認証DELETEは401「認証が必要です。」 */
   172	    public function test_destroy_unauthenticated_returns_401(): void
   173	    {
   174	        $book = $this->makeBook();
   175	
   176	        $this->deleteJson("/api/v1/books/{$book->id}")
   177	            ->assertStatus(401)
   178	            ->assertJson(['message' => '認証が必要です。']);
   179	    }
   180	
   181	    // ================= G-3: 他人の書籍への書き込みは403（メソッドごと個別） =================
   182	
   183	    /** G-3: 他人の書籍へのPUTは403「この操作を実行する権限がありません。」 */
   184	    public function test_update_other_users_book_returns_403(): void
   185	    {
   186	        $owner = User::factory()->create();
   187	        $other = User::factory()->create();
   188	        $book = $this->makeBook(['user_id' => $owner->id]);
   189	        Sanctum::actingAs($other);
   190	
   191	        $this->putJson("/api/v1/books/{$book->id}", $this->validPayload())
   192	            ->assertStatus(403)
   193	            ->assertJson(['message' => 'この操作を実行する権限がありません。']);
   194	
   195	        $this->assertDatabaseHas('books', ['id' => $book->id, 'user_id' => $owner->id]);
   196	    }
   197	
   198	    /** G-3: 他人の書籍へのDELETEは403「この操作を実行する権限がありません。」 */
   199	    public function test_destroy_other_users_book_returns_403(): void
   200	    {
   201	        $owner = User::factory()->create();
   202	        $other = User::factory()->create();
   203	        $book = $this->makeBook(['user_id' => $owner->id]);
   204	        Sanctum::actingAs($other);
   205	
   206	        $this->deleteJson("/api/v1/books/{$book->id}")
   207	            ->assertStatus(403)
   208	            ->assertJson(['message' => 'この操作を実行する権限がありません。']);
   209	
   210	        $this->assertDatabaseHas('books', ['id' => $book->id, 'deleted_at' => null]);
   211	    }
   212	
   213	    // ================= 応用の周辺確認（読み取り系の挙動） =================
   214	
   215	    /** GET詳細：存在しないIDは404 JSON（非破壊） */
   216	    public function test_show_not_found_returns_json_404(): void
   217	    {
   218	        $this->getJson('/api/v1/books/99999')
   219	            ->assertStatus(404)
   220	            ->assertJson(['message' => '指定された書籍が見つかりません。']);
   221	    }
   222	
   223	    /** GET詳細：論理削除済みIDは404（一覧・詳細のGETは標準除外挙動） */
   224	    public function test_show_soft_deleted_returns_404(): void
   225	    {
   226	        $book = $this->makeBook();
   227	        $book->delete();
   228	
   229	        $this->getJson("/api/v1/books/{$book->id}")->assertStatus(404);
   230	    }
   231	
   232	    /** POST：認証済みでも入力不正は422 */
   233	    public function test_store_validation_error_when_authenticated(): void
   234	    {
   235	        Sanctum::actingAs(User::factory()->create());
   236	
   237	        $this->postJson('/api/v1/books', [])
   238	            ->assertStatus(422)
   239	            ->assertJsonStructure(['message', 'errors']);
   240	    }
   241	}

===== tests/Feature/AuthTest.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Tests\Feature;
     4	
     5	use App\Models\User;
     6	use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
     7	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
     8	use Tests\TestCase;
     9	
    10	class AuthTest extends TestCase
    11	{
    12	    use RefreshDatabase;
    13	
    14	    /** F-A5: 会員登録すると自動ログイン後books.indexへ */
    15	    public function test_registration_logs_in_and_redirects(): void
    16	    {
    17	        $response = $this->post('/register', [
    18	            'name' => '新規太郎',
    19	            'email' => 'new@example.com',
    20	            'password' => 'password',
    21	            'password_confirmation' => 'password',
    22	        ]);
    23	
    24	        $response->assertRedirect('/books');
    25	        $this->assertAuthenticated();
    26	        $this->assertDatabaseHas('users', ['email' => 'new@example.com']);
    27	    }
    28	
    29	    /** F-A6: 登録バリデーション */
    30	    public function test_registration_validation_errors(): void
    31	    {
    32	        // 必須未入力
    33	        $this->post('/register', [])
    34	            ->assertSessionHasErrors(['name', 'email', 'password']);
    35	
    36	        // メール形式不正
    37	        $this->post('/register', [
    38	            'name' => 'a',
    39	            'email' => 'invalid',
    40	            'password' => 'password',
    41	            'password_confirmation' => 'password',
    42	        ])->assertSessionHasErrors('email');
    43	
    44	        // パスワード不一致
    45	        $this->post('/register', [
    46	            'name' => 'a',
    47	            'email' => 'ok@example.com',
    48	            'password' => 'password',
    49	            'password_confirmation' => 'different',
    50	        ])->assertSessionHasErrors('password');
    51	    }
    52	
    53	    /** F-A7: ログイン成功 */
    54	    public function test_login_success(): void
    55	    {
    56	        $user = User::factory()->create(['password' => Hash::make('password')]);
    57	
    58	        $response = $this->post('/login', [
    59	            'email' => $user->email,
    60	            'password' => 'password',
    61	        ]);
    62	
    63	        $response->assertRedirect('/books');
    64	        $this->assertAuthenticatedAs($user);
    65	    }
    66	
    67	    /** F-A8: ログイン失敗メッセージ */
    68	    public function test_login_failure_message(): void
    69	    {
    70	        $user = User::factory()->create(['password' => Hash::make('password')]);
    71	
    72	        $response = $this->from('/login')->post('/login', [
    73	            'email' => $user->email,
    74	            'password' => 'wrong',
    75	        ]);
    76	
    77	        $response->assertSessionHasErrors('email');
    78	        $this->assertGuest();
    79	        $errors = session('errors');
    80	        $this->assertSame('メールアドレスまたはパスワードが正しくありません', $errors->first('email'));
    81	    }
    82	
    83	    /** F-A9: ログアウト後は認証必須画面でログインが要求される */
    84	    public function test_logout_requires_login_again(): void
    85	    {
    86	        $user = User::factory()->create();
    87	
    88	        $this->actingAs($user)->post('/logout');
    89	        $this->assertGuest();

    92	    }

===== tests/Feature/BookCrudAdvancedTest.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Tests\Feature;
     4	
     5	use App\Models\Book;
     6	use App\Models\Genre;
     7	use App\Models\User;
     8	use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
     9	use Tests\TestCase;
    10	
    11	/**
    12	 * 走行⑦〜⑧：書籍CRUDの応用（isbn/published_dateのnullable化・認可詳細）。検品表 H。
    13	 * Web側の書籍登録・更新では isbn・published_date は任意（nullable）。
    14	 */
    15	class BookCrudAdvancedTest extends TestCase
    16	{
    17	    use RefreshDatabase;
    18	
    19	    /**
    20	     * 有効な書籍登録ペイロードを返す。
    21	     *
    22	     * @return array<string, mixed>
    23	     */
    24	    private function validPayload(array $genreIds, array $overrides = []): array
    25	    {
    26	        return array_merge([
    27	            'title' => 'テスト書籍',
    28	            'author' => 'テスト著者',
    29	            'isbn' => '9784000000001',
    30	            'published_date' => '2020-01-01',
    31	            'description' => '説明文',
    32	            'image_url' => 'https://example.com/a.jpg',
    33	            'genres' => $genreIds,
    34	        ], $overrides);
    35	    }
    36	
    37	    /** 書籍登録フォーム表示：ジャンル選択肢を含む（BookController::create） */
    38	    public function test_create_form_displayed_with_genres(): void
    39	    {
    40	        $user = User::factory()->create();
    41	        Genre::factory()->create(['name' => '登録フォームのジャンル']);
    42

    44	            ->assertOk()
    45	            ->assertSee('登録フォームのジャンル');
    46	    }

    48	    /** 書籍登録フォームは未認証だと/loginへリダイレクト */

    50	    {
    51	        $this->get('/books/create')->assertRedirect('/login');
    52	    }
    53	
    54	    /** H-1: isbn・published_dateを空のまま登録が成功する（nullable） */
    55	    public function test_store_succeeds_with_empty_isbn_and_published_date(): void
    56	    {
    57	        $user = User::factory()->create();
    58	        $genre = Genre::factory()->create();
    59	
    60	        $response = $this->actingAs($user)->post('/books', $this->validPayload([$genre->id], [
    61	            'isbn' => '',
    62	            'published_date' => '',
    63	        ]));
    64	
    65	        $book = Book::first();
    66	        $response->assertRedirect(route('books.show', $book));
    67	        $response->assertSessionHasNoErrors();
    68	        $this->assertDatabaseHas('books', [
    69	            'id' => $book->id,
    70	            'title' => 'テスト書籍',
    71	            'isbn' => null,
    72	            'published_date' => null,
    73	        ]);
    74	    }
    75	
    76	    /** H-1: isbn・published_dateを空のまま更新が成功する（nullable） */
    77	    public function test_update_succeeds_with_empty_isbn_and_published_date(): void
    78	    {
    79	        $owner = User::factory()->create();
    80	        $genre = Genre::factory()->create();
    81	        $book = Book::factory()->create(['user_id' => $owner->id, 'isbn' => '9784000000055']);
    82	        $book->genres()->sync([$genre->id]);
    83	
    84	        $response = $this->actingAs($owner)->put(route('books.update', $book), $this->validPayload([$genre->id], [
    85	            'isbn' => '',
    86	            'published_date' => '',
    87	            'title' => '更新後タイトル',
    88	        ]));
    89	
    90	        $response->assertRedirect(route('books.show', $book));
    91	        $response->assertSessionHasNoErrors();
    92	        $this->assertDatabaseHas('books', [
    93	            'id' => $book->id,
    94	            'title' => '更新後タイトル',
    95	            'isbn' => null,
    96	            'published_date' => null,
    97	        ]);
    98	    }
    99	
   100	    /** H-2: isbnに値がある場合のみ桁数チェックが働く（13桁でない→エラー） */
   101	    public function test_isbn_format_validated_only_when_present(): void
   102	    {
   103	        $user = User::factory()->create();
   104	        $genre = Genre::factory()->create();
   105	
   106	        $this->actingAs($user)->post('/books', $this->validPayload([$genre->id], ['isbn' => '123']))
   107	            ->assertSessionHasErrors(['isbn' => 'ISBNは13桁の数字で入力してください（入力がある場合のみ）']);
   108	    }
   109	
   110	    /** H-2: isbnに値がある場合のみ一意性チェックが働く（重複→エラー） */
   111	    public function test_isbn_uniqueness_validated_only_when_present(): void
   112	    {
   113	        $user = User::factory()->create();
   114	        $genre = Genre::factory()->create();
   115	        Book::factory()->create(['isbn' => '9784000000009']);
   116	
   117	        $this->actingAs($user)->post('/books', $this->validPayload([$genre->id], ['isbn' => '9784000000009']))
   118	            ->assertSessionHasErrors(['isbn' => 'このISBNは既に登録されています']);
   119	    }
   120	
   121	    /** H-2: published_dateに値がある場合のみ日付形式チェックが働く（不正→エラー） */
   122	    public function test_published_date_format_validated_only_when_present(): void
   123	    {
   124	        $user = User::factory()->create();
   125	        $genre = Genre::factory()->create();

   129	    }
   130	
   131	    /** H-3: 空POSTでも isbn/published_date には必須エラーが立たない（title/author/genresのみ必須） */
   132	    public function test_empty_post_does_not_require_isbn_or_published_date(): void
   133	    {
   134	        $user = User::factory()->create();
   135

   137

   139	        $response->assertSessionDoesntHaveErrors(['isbn', 'published_date']);
   140	    }

   152	    /** H-4: 他人の書籍の更新（PUT）は403・レコード不変 */
   153	    public function test_other_user_cannot_update(): void
   154	    {
   155	        $owner = User::factory()->create();
   156	        $other = User::factory()->create();

   158	        $book = Book::factory()->create(['user_id' => $owner->id, 'title' => '元タイトル']);
   159	        $book->genres()->sync([$genre->id]);
   160	
   161	        $this->actingAs($other)->put(route('books.update', $book), $this->validPayload([$genre->id], ['title' => '乗っ取り']))
   162	            ->assertForbidden();
   163	        $this->assertDatabaseHas('books', ['id' => $book->id, 'title' => '元タイトル']);
   164	    }
   165	
   166	    /** H-4: 他人の書籍の削除（DELETE）は403・削除されない */
   167	    public function test_other_user_cannot_delete(): void
   168	    {

   171	        $book = Book::factory()->create(['user_id' => $owner->id]);
   172	
   173	        $this->actingAs($other)->delete(route('books.destroy', $book))->assertForbidden();
   174	        $this->assertNull($book->fresh()->deleted_at);
   175	    }

===== tests/Feature/BookCrudTest.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Tests\Feature;
     4	
     5	use App\Models\Book;
     6	use App\Models\Genre;
     7	use App\Models\Review;
     8	use App\Models\User;
     9	use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
    10	use Tests\TestCase;
    11	
    12	class BookCrudTest extends TestCase
    13	{
    14	    use RefreshDatabase;
    15	
    16	    /**
    17	     * 有効な書籍登録ペイロードを返す。
    18	     *
    19	     * @return array<string, mixed>
    20	     */
    21	    private function validPayload(array $genreIds, array $overrides = []): array
    22	    {
    23	        return array_merge([
    24	            'title' => 'テスト書籍',
    25	            'author' => 'テスト著者',
    26	            'isbn' => '9784000000001',
    27	            'published_date' => '2020-01-01',
    28	            'description' => '説明文',
    29	            'image_url' => 'https://example.com/a.jpg',
    30	            'genres' => $genreIds,
    31	        ], $overrides);
    32	    }
    33	
    34	    /** F-B1: 登録成功 */
    35	    public function test_store_book_success(): void
    36	    {
    37	        $user = User::factory()->create();
    38	        $genre = Genre::factory()->create();
    39	
    40	        $response = $this->actingAs($user)->post('/books', $this->validPayload([$genre->id]));
    41	
    42	        $book = Book::first();
    43	        $response->assertRedirect(route('books.show', $book));
    44	        $response->assertSessionHas('success', '書籍を登録しました');
    45	        $this->assertDatabaseHas('books', ['title' => 'テスト書籍', 'user_id' => $user->id]);
    46	    }
    47	
    48	    /** F-B2: 登録バリデーション */
    49	    public function test_store_validation_errors(): void
    50	    {
    51	        $user = User::factory()->create();
    52	        $genre = Genre::factory()->create();
    53	
    54	        // 必須未入力・genre未選択（isbn/published_dateは応用でnullable化のため必須エラーは立たない）
    55	        $this->actingAs($user)->post('/books', [])
    56	            ->assertSessionHasErrors(['title', 'author', 'genres'])
    57	            ->assertSessionDoesntHaveErrors(['isbn', 'published_date']);
    58	
    59	        // isbn 13桁でない
    60	        $this->actingAs($user)->post('/books', $this->validPayload([$genre->id], ['isbn' => '123']))
    61	            ->assertSessionHasErrors('isbn');
    62	
    63	        // isbn 重複
    64	        Book::factory()->create(['isbn' => '9784000000009']);
    65	        $this->actingAs($user)->post('/books', $this->validPayload([$genre->id], ['isbn' => '9784000000009']))
    66	            ->assertSessionHasErrors('isbn');
    67	    }
    68	
    69	    /** F-B3: ジャンル紐付けsync（登録・編集） */
    70	    public function test_genre_sync_on_store_and_update(): void
    71	    {
    72	        $user = User::factory()->create();
    73	        $genres = Genre::factory()->count(3)->create();
    74	
    75	        $this->actingAs($user)->post('/books', $this->validPayload([$genres[0]->id, $genres[1]->id]));
    76	        $book = Book::first();
    77	        $this->assertEqualsCanonicalizing(
    78	            [$genres[0]->id, $genres[1]->id],
    79	            $book->genres->pluck('id')->all()
    80	        );
    81	
    82	        // 編集で別ジャンルのみにsync
    83	        $this->actingAs($user)->put(route('books.update', $book), $this->validPayload([$genres[2]->id]));
    84	        $this->assertEqualsCanonicalizing([$genres[2]->id], $book->fresh()->genres->pluck('id')->all());
    85	    }
    86	
    87	    /** F-B4: 編集の認可 */
    88	    public function test_edit_authorization(): void
    89	    {
    90	        $owner = User::factory()->create();
    91	        $other = User::factory()->create();
    92	        $book = Book::factory()->create(['user_id' => $owner->id]);
    93	
    94	        $this->actingAs($owner)->get(route('books.edit', $book))->assertOk();
    95	        $this->actingAs($other)->get(route('books.edit', $book))->assertForbidden();
    96	    }
    97	
    98	    /** F-B5: 削除の認可 */
    99	    public function test_delete_authorization(): void
   100	    {
   101	        $owner = User::factory()->create();
   102	        $other = User::factory()->create();
   103	        $book = Book::factory()->create(['user_id' => $owner->id]);
   104	
   105	        $this->actingAs($other)->delete(route('books.destroy', $book))->assertForbidden();
   106	        $this->assertNull($book->fresh()->deleted_at);
   107	
   108	        $this->actingAs($owner)->delete(route('books.destroy', $book))
   109	            ->assertRedirect(route('books.index'));
   110	        $this->assertNotNull($book->fresh()->deleted_at);
   111	    }
   112	
   113	    /** F-B6: 削除後の一覧・ランキング・API除外 */

   115	    {

===== tests/Feature/BookFormUiTest.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Tests\Feature;
     4	
     5	use App\Models\Genre;
     6	use App\Models\User;
     7	use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
     8	use Tests\TestCase;
     9	
    10	/**
    11	 * 書籍一覧の検索フォームと、登録画面のISBN自動入力セクションが
    12	 * 画面に描画されることを確認する（デザイン画像1・2準拠）。
    13	 * コントローラ挙動ではなくUI要素の存在を検証する。
    14	 */
    15	class BookFormUiTest extends TestCase
    16	{
    17	    use RefreshDatabase;
    18	
    19	    /** 一覧画面に検索フォーム（キーワード・ジャンル・並び順＋検索/リセット/登録）が描画される */
    20	    public function test_index_renders_search_form(): void
    21	    {
    22	        $genre = Genre::factory()->create(['name' => 'テストジャンル']);
    23	
    24	        $response = $this->get('/books');
    25	
    26	        $response->assertOk();
    27	        // 検索フォームと入力要素
    28	        $response->assertSee('<form method="GET"', false);
    29	        $response->assertSee('name="keyword"', false);
    30	        $response->assertSee('name="genre"', false);
    31	        $response->assertSee('name="sort"', false);
    32	        // ラベルとボタン
    33	        $response->assertSee('キーワード');
    34	        $response->assertSee('並び順');
    35	        $response->assertSee('検索');
    36	        $response->assertSee('リセット');
    37	        $response->assertSee('書籍を登録');
    38	        // ジャンルselectに登録ジャンルが並ぶ／並び順の選択肢
    39	        $response->assertSee('すべて');
    40	        $response->assertSee('テストジャンル');
    41	        $response->assertSeeInOrder(['新しい順', '古い順', 'タイトル順', '評価が高い順']);
    42	    }
    43	
    44	    /** 検索実行後、フォームに入力値が保持される */
    45	    public function test_search_form_retains_input_values(): void
    46	    {
    47	        $genre = Genre::factory()->create(['name' => '保持ジャンル']);
    48	
    49	        $response = $this->get('/books?keyword=あいう&genre='.$genre->id.'&sort=title');
    50	
    51	        $response->assertOk();
    52	        $response->assertSee('value="あいう"', false);
    53	        // 選択中のジャンル・並び順にselectedが付く
    54	        $response->assertSee('value="'.$genre->id.'" selected', false);
    55	        $response->assertSee('value="title" selected', false);
    56	    }
    57

    60	    {

    64	
    65	        $response->assertOk();

    68	        $response->assertSee('id="isbn_search"', false);
    69	        $response->assertSee('id="isbn_search_button"', false);
    70	        // 自動入力用に searchByIsbn エンドポイントを叩くスクリプトが埋め込まれている

===== tests/Feature/BookSearchTest.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Tests\Feature;
     4	
     5	use App\Models\Book;
     6	use App\Models\Genre;
     7	use App\Models\Review;
     8	use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
     9	use Tests\TestCase;
    10	
    11	/**
    12	 * 走行⑦：書籍一覧のキーワード検索・ジャンル絞り込み・並び替え（検品表 D）。
    13	 */
    14	class BookSearchTest extends TestCase
    15	{
    16	    use RefreshDatabase;
    17	
    18	    /**
    19	     * ジャンルを紐付けた書籍を作成するヘルパ。
    20	     */
    21	    private function makeBook(array $attrs, ?Genre $genre = null): Book
    22	    {
    23	        $book = Book::factory()->create($attrs);
    24	        if ($genre !== null) {
    25	            $book->genres()->sync([$genre->id]);
    26	        }
    27	
    28	        return $book;
    29	    }
    30	
    31	    /** D-1: キーワード部分一致（タイトル・著者）で該当書籍のみが返る */
    32	    public function test_keyword_partial_match_returns_only_matching_books(): void
    33	    {
    34	        $this->makeBook(['title' => 'Laravel実践入門', 'author' => '田中太郎']);
    35	        $this->makeBook(['title' => 'Python基礎講座', 'author' => '佐藤花子']);
    36	
    37	        // タイトル部分一致
    38	        $this->get('/books?keyword=Laravel')
    39	            ->assertOk()
    40	            ->assertSee('Laravel実践入門')
    41	            ->assertDontSee('Python基礎講座');
    42	
    43	        // 著者部分一致
    44	        $this->get('/books?keyword=佐藤')
    45	            ->assertOk()
    46	            ->assertSee('Python基礎講座')
    47	            ->assertDontSee('Laravel実践入門');
    48	    }
    49	
    50	    /** D-2: ジャンル絞り込みで該当書籍のみが返る */
    51	    public function test_genre_filter_returns_only_matching_books(): void
    52	    {
    53	        $tech = Genre::factory()->create(['name' => '技術書']);
    54	        $novel = Genre::factory()->create(['name' => '小説']);
    55	
    56	        $this->makeBook(['title' => '技術書のほう'], $tech);
    57	        $this->makeBook(['title' => '小説のほう'], $novel);
    58	
    59	        $this->get('/books?genre='.$tech->id)
    60	            ->assertOk()
    61	            ->assertSee('技術書のほう')
    62	            ->assertDontSee('小説のほう');
    63	    }
    64	
    65	    /** D-3: 並び替えが指定順で返る（title昇順とnewestで順序が変わる） */
    66	    public function test_sort_returns_books_in_specified_order(): void
    67	    {
    68	        // created_atを明示し、title順とnewest順が明確に食い違うようにする。
    69	        $this->makeBook(['title' => 'AAA Book', 'created_at' => now()->subDays(3)]);
    70	        $this->makeBook(['title' => 'MMM Book', 'created_at' => now()->subDays(2)]);
    71	        $this->makeBook(['title' => 'ZZZ Book', 'created_at' => now()->subDay()]);
    72	
    73	        // title昇順：AAA→MMM→ZZZ
    74	        $this->get('/books?sort=title')
    75	            ->assertOk()
    76	            ->assertSeeInOrder(['AAA Book', 'MMM Book', 'ZZZ Book']);
    77	
    78	        // newest（作成日時の降順）：ZZZ→MMM→AAA（titleとは逆順）
    79	        $this->get('/books?sort=newest')
    80	            ->assertOk()
    81	            ->assertSeeInOrder(['ZZZ Book', 'MMM Book', 'AAA Book']);
    82	    }
    83	
    84	    /** D-3補: sort=oldest は作成の古い順で返る */
    85	    public function test_sort_oldest_returns_ascending_by_created_at(): void
    86	    {
    87	        $this->makeBook(['title' => '一番古い本', 'created_at' => now()->subDays(3)]);
    88	        $this->makeBook(['title' => '中間の本', 'created_at' => now()->subDays(2)]);
    89	        $this->makeBook(['title' => '一番新しい本', 'created_at' => now()->subDay()]);
    90	
    91	        $this->get('/books?sort=oldest')
    92	            ->assertOk()
    93	            ->assertSeeInOrder(['一番古い本', '中間の本', '一番新しい本']);
    94	    }
    95	
    96	    /** D-3補: sort=rating は平均評価の高い順で返る */
    97	    public function test_sort_rating_returns_by_average_rating_desc(): void
    98	    {
    99	        $high = $this->makeBook(['title' => '高評価ソート本']);
   100	        Review::factory()->create(['book_id' => $high->id, 'rating' => 5]);
   101	        $low = $this->makeBook(['title' => '低評価ソート本']);
   102	        Review::factory()->create(['book_id' => $low->id, 'rating' => 1]);
   103	
   104	        $this->get('/books?sort=rating')
   105	            ->assertOk()
   106	            ->assertSeeInOrder(['高評価ソート本', '低評価ソート本']);
   107	    }
   108	
   109	    /** D-4: 検索条件を維持したままページ送りできる（withQueryStringで条件保持） */
   110	    public function test_search_condition_preserved_across_pagination(): void
   111	    {
   112	        // キーワード一致書籍を11件作成し2ページに分割させる。
   113	        for ($i = 1; $i <= 11; $i++) {
   114	            $this->makeBook(['title' => "ZZUNIQUE {$i}"]);
   115	        }
   116	
   117	        $response = $this->get('/books?keyword=ZZUNIQUE');
   118	        $response->assertOk();
   119	        // ページネーションリンクにkeywordとpage=2が引き継がれている。
   120	        $response->assertSee('keyword=ZZUNIQUE', false);
   121	        $response->assertSee('page=2', false);
   122	    }
   123	
   124	    /** 論理削除済み書籍は一覧・検索から除外される（CLAUDE.md §9-2） */
   125	    public function test_soft_deleted_book_excluded_from_search(): void
   126	    {
   127	        $book = $this->makeBook(['title' => '削除される本KEYWORDX']);
   128	        $book->delete();
   129	
   130	        $this->get('/books?keyword=KEYWORDX')
   131	            ->assertOk()
   132	            ->assertDontSee('削除される本KEYWORDX');
   133	    }
   134

===== tests/Feature/ExpireReadingPlansTest.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Tests\Feature;
     4	
     5	use App\Enums\ReadingPlanStatus;
     6	use App\Models\Book;
     7	use App\Models\ReadingPlan;
     8	use App\Models\User;
     9	use Carbon\Carbon;
    10	use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
    11	use Tests\TestCase;
    12	
    13	/**
    14	 * 走行⑪：自動失効バッチ reading-plans:expire（検品表 J）。
    15	 * 対象は「本日の3日前ちょうど・in_progress」のみ（等値実装）。時刻は setTestNow で固定する。
    16	 */
    17	class ExpireReadingPlansTest extends TestCase
    18	{
    19	    use RefreshDatabase;
    20	
    21	    protected function setUp(): void
    22	    {
    23	        parent::setUp();
    24	        // J-5: 時刻依存を避けるため today を固定する。
    25	        Carbon::setTestNow(Carbon::parse('2026-09-17'));
    26	    }
    27	
    28	    protected function tearDown(): void
    29	    {
    30	        Carbon::setTestNow(null);
    31	        parent::tearDown();
    32	    }
    33	
    34	    private function makePlan(ReadingPlanStatus $status, string $targetDate, ?string $completedAt = null): ReadingPlan
    35	    {
    36	        return ReadingPlan::create([
    37	            'user_id' => User::factory()->create()->id,
    38	            'book_id' => Book::factory()->create()->id,
    39	            'target_date' => $targetDate,
    40	            'status' => $status,
    41	            'completed_at' => $completedAt,
    42	        ]);
    43	    }
    44	
    45	    /** J-1: 本日の3日前ちょうど・in_progress が expired に変わる */
    46	    public function test_exactly_three_days_ago_in_progress_becomes_expired(): void
    47	    {
    48	        $plan = $this->makePlan(ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress, Carbon::today()->subDays(3)->toDateString());
    49	
    50	        $this->artisan('reading-plans:expire')->assertExitCode(0);
    51	
    52	        $this->assertSame(ReadingPlanStatus::Expired, $plan->fresh()->status);
    53	    }
    54	
    55	    /** J-2: 本日の4日前・in_progress は in_progress のまま（等値実装を強制） */
    56	    public function test_four_days_ago_in_progress_stays_in_progress(): void
    57	    {
    58	        $plan = $this->makePlan(ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress, Carbon::today()->subDays(4)->toDateString());
    59	
    60	        $this->artisan('reading-plans:expire')->assertExitCode(0);
    61	
    62	        $this->assertSame(ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress, $plan->fresh()->status);
    63	    }
    64	
    65	    /** J-3: 本日の2日前・当日・未来は変わらない */
    66	    public function test_two_days_ago_today_and_future_stay_in_progress(): void
    67	    {
    68	        $twoDaysAgo = $this->makePlan(ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress, Carbon::today()->subDays(2)->toDateString());
    69	        $today = $this->makePlan(ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress, Carbon::today()->toDateString());
    70	        $future = $this->makePlan(ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress, Carbon::today()->addDays(3)->toDateString());
    71	
    72	        $this->artisan('reading-plans:expire')->assertExitCode(0);
    73	
    74	        $this->assertSame(ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress, $twoDaysAgo->fresh()->status);
    75	        $this->assertSame(ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress, $today->fresh()->status);
    76	        $this->assertSame(ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress, $future->fresh()->status);
    77	    }
    78	
    79	    /** J-4: 完了済み（3日前）は completed のまま、completed_at も書き換わらない */
    80	    public function test_completed_plan_unchanged(): void
    81	    {
    82	        $completedAt = '2026-09-10 12:00:00';
    83	        $plan = $this->makePlan(ReadingPlanStatus::Completed, Carbon::today()->subDays(3)->toDateString(), $completedAt);
    84	
    85	        $this->artisan('reading-plans:expire')->assertExitCode(0);
    86	
    87	        $fresh = $plan->fresh();
    88	        $this->assertSame(ReadingPlanStatus::Completed, $fresh->status);
    89	        $this->assertSame($completedAt, $fresh->completed_at->toDateTimeString());
    90	    }
    91	}

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Tests\Feature;
     4	
     5	use App\Models\Book;
     6	use App\Models\User;
     7	use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;

    10	class FavoriteTest extends TestCase

    14	    /** F-F1: トグルON */

    22	    }

    30

    43	    }

    47	    {

    49	    }
    50	
    51	    /** F-F5: 一覧は自分のお気に入りのみ */
    52	    public function test_index_shows_only_own_favorites(): void
    53	    {
    54	        $user = User::factory()->create();
    55	        $other = User::factory()->create();
    56	        $mine = Book::factory()->create(['title' => 'マイお気に入り本']);
    57	        $theirs = Book::factory()->create(['title' => '他人のお気に入り本']);
    58	
    59	        $user->favoriteBooks()->attach($mine->id);
    60	        $other->favoriteBooks()->attach($theirs->id);
    61	
    62	        $this->actingAs($user)->get('/favorites')
    63	            ->assertOk()
    64	            ->assertSee('マイお気に入り本')
    65	            ->assertDontSee('他人のお気に入り本');
    66	    }
    67	
    68	    /** D-6: 一覧は登録した書籍の作成日時が新しい順 */
    69	    public function test_index_ordered_by_created_at_desc(): void
    70	    {
    71	        $user = User::factory()->create();
    72	        $older = Book::factory()->create(['title' => '古いお気に入り本', 'created_at' => now()->subDay()]);
    73	        $newer = Book::factory()->create(['title' => '新しいお気に入り本', 'created_at' => now()]);
    74	
    75	        $user->favoriteBooks()->attach([$older->id, $newer->id]);
    76	
    77	        $this->actingAs($user)->get('/favorites')
    78	            ->assertOk()
    79	            ->assertSeeInOrder([$newer->title, $older->title]);
    80	    }
    81	
    82	    /** E-1 / E-3: 削除済み書籍は一覧から除外され、復元で再表示される */
    83	    public function test_deleted_book_excluded_and_restored_book_reappears(): void
    84	    {
    85	        $user = User::factory()->create();
    86	        $book = Book::factory()->create(['title' => 'お気に入り削除復元本']);
    87	        $user->favoriteBooks()->attach($book->id);
    88	
    89	        // 登録直後は表示される
    90	        $this->actingAs($user)->get('/favorites')->assertSee($book->title);
    91	
    92	        // E-1: 削除すると一覧から除外される
    93	        $book->delete();
    94	        $this->actingAs($user)->get('/favorites')->assertDontSee($book->title);
    95	
    96	        // E-3: 復元すると一覧に再表示される
    97	        $book->restore();
    98	        $this->actingAs($user)->get('/favorites')->assertSee($book->title);
    99	    }
   100	}

   120

   126	    /** B-2: 削除済み書籍のみ紐づくジャンルは一覧で0冊表示 */
   127	    public function test_index_shows_zero_count_for_genre_with_only_trashed_books(): void
   128	    {
   129	        $user = User::factory()->create();
   130	        $genre = Genre::factory()->create(['name' => '削除済みのみジャンル']);
   131	        $book = Book::factory()->create();
   132	        $book->genres()->sync([$genre->id]);
   133	        $book->delete();
   134	
   135	        $this->actingAs($user)->get(route('genres.index'))
   136	            ->assertOk()
   137	            ->assertSee('削除済みのみジャンル')
   138	            ->assertSee('0冊');
   139	    }
   140	
   141	    /** B-3: 詳細の所属書籍は10件ページネーション */
   142	    public function test_show_paginates_books_at_10(): void
   143	    {
   144	        $user = User::factory()->create();
   145	        $genre = Genre::factory()->create();
   146	        $books = Book::factory()->count(11)->create();
   147	        foreach ($books as $book) {
   148	            $book->genres()->sync([$genre->id]);
   149	        }
   150	
   151	        $response = $this->actingAs($user)->get(route('genres.show', $genre));
   152	        $response->assertOk();
   153	        $this->assertTrue($response->viewData('books')->hasMorePages());
   154	    }
   155	}

===== tests/Feature/IsbnSearchTest.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Tests\Feature;
     4

    11	/**

    18	
    19	    /** E-1 / E-2: ISBN13桁ちょうどの正常系。Http::fake()で外部APIをモックする。 */

    21	    {
    22	        Http::fake([
    23	            'www.googleapis.com/*' => Http::response([
    24	                'totalItems' => 1,

    48	            ]);
    49

    52	    }

   142

   144	        $this->actingAs($user)->getJson('/books/isbn/9784000000001')->assertOk();

   147	    }
