   156	                'items' => [['volumeInfo' => ['title' => 'X']]],
   157	            ], 200),
   158	        ]);
   159	
   160	        $user = User::factory()->create();
   161	        $this->actingAs($user)->getJson('/books/isbn/9784000000001')->assertOk();
   162	
   163	        Http::assertSent(fn ($request) => ! str_contains($request->url(), 'key='));
   164	    }
   165	}

===== tests/Feature/NotificationTest.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Tests\Feature;
     4	
     5	use App\Enums\NotificationTiming;
     6	use App\Enums\ReadingPlanStatus;
     7	use App\Models\Book;
     8	use App\Models\ReadingPlan;
     9	use App\Models\User;
    10	use App\Notifications\ReadingPlanReminder;
    11	use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
    12	use Tests\TestCase;
    13	
    14	/**
    15	 * 走行⑫：通知一覧の表示・既読化（検品表⑭差し戻し 1）。
    16	 */
    17	class NotificationTest extends TestCase
    18	{
    19	    use RefreshDatabase;
    20	
    21	    /**
    22	     * 指定ユーザーに読書計画リマインダー通知を1件作成し、そのDatabaseNotificationを返す。
    23	     */
    24	    private function notifyUser(User $user, NotificationTiming $timing, string $bookTitle): \Illuminate\Notifications\DatabaseNotification
    25	    {
    26	        $plan = ReadingPlan::create([
    27	            'user_id' => $user->id,
    28	            'book_id' => Book::factory()->create(['title' => $bookTitle])->id,
    29	            'target_date' => now()->addDays(3)->toDateString(),
    30	            'status' => ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress,
    31	        ]);
    32	
    33	        $user->notify(new ReadingPlanReminder($plan, $timing));
    34	
    35	        return $user->fresh()->notifications()->latest()->first();
    36	    }
    37	
    38	    /** 通知一覧：自分の通知のみ・新しい順に表示される */
    39	    public function test_index_shows_own_notifications_newest_first(): void
    40	    {
    41	        $user = User::factory()->create();
    42	        $other = User::factory()->create();
    43	
    44	        $older = $this->notifyUser($user, NotificationTiming::ThreeDaysBefore, '古い通知の本');
    45	        $newer = $this->notifyUser($user, NotificationTiming::OnDueDate, '新しい通知の本');
    46	        // created_at を明示して並び順を確定させる。
    47	        $older->forceFill(['created_at' => now()->subMinutes(10)])->save();
    48	        $newer->forceFill(['created_at' => now()])->save();
    49	
    50	        // 他人の通知（一覧に出てはいけない）
    51	        $this->notifyUser($other, NotificationTiming::ThreeDaysBefore, '他人の通知の本');
    52	
    53	        $response = $this->actingAs($user)->get('/notifications');
    54	        $response->assertOk();
    55	
    56	        // 自分の通知は2件、新しい順（新しい通知→古い通知）。
    57	        $this->assertCount(2, $response->viewData('notifications'));
    58	        $response->assertSeeInOrder(['新しい通知の本', '古い通知の本']);
    59	        // 他人の通知は表示されない。
    60	        $response->assertDontSee('他人の通知の本');
    61	    }
    62	
    63	    /** 既読化：本人は既読にでき、read_atが入る */
    64	    public function test_owner_can_mark_notification_read(): void
    65	    {
    66	        $user = User::factory()->create();
    67	        $notification = $this->notifyUser($user, NotificationTiming::OnDueDate, '既読にする本');
    68	
    69	        $this->assertNull($notification->read_at);
    70	
    71	        $this->actingAs($user)
    72	            ->from('/notifications')
    73	            ->post("/notifications/{$notification->id}/read")
    74	            ->assertRedirect('/notifications');
    75	
    76	        $this->assertNotNull($notification->fresh()->read_at);
    77	    }
    78	
    79	    /** 既読化：他人の通知は既読化できない（403）・read_atは変わらない */
    80	    public function test_other_user_cannot_mark_notification_read(): void
    81	    {
    82	        $owner = User::factory()->create();
    83	        $other = User::factory()->create();
    84	        $notification = $this->notifyUser($owner, NotificationTiming::OnDueDate, '他人が触れない本');
    85	
    86	        $this->actingAs($other)
    87	            ->post("/notifications/{$notification->id}/read")
    88	            ->assertForbidden();
    89	
    90	        $this->assertNull($notification->fresh()->read_at);
    91	    }
    92	
    93	    /** 認可：未認証で通知一覧を叩くと/loginへリダイレクト */
    94	    public function test_guest_redirected_from_notifications(): void
    95	    {
    96	        $this->get('/notifications')->assertRedirect('/login');
    97	    }
    98	}

===== tests/Feature/RankingTest.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Tests\Feature;
     4	
     5	use App\Models\Book;
     6	use App\Models\Review;
     7	use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
     8	use Tests\TestCase;
     9	
    10	class RankingTest extends TestCase
    11	{
    12	    use RefreshDatabase;
    13	
    14	    /** F-K1: 平均評価降順でTOP10 */
    15	    public function test_ranking_ordered_by_avg_rating_desc(): void
    16	    {
    17	        $low = Book::factory()->create(['title' => '低評価本']);
    18	        $high = Book::factory()->create(['title' => '高評価本']);
    19	        Review::factory()->create(['book_id' => $low->id, 'rating' => 3]);
    20	        Review::factory()->create(['book_id' => $high->id, 'rating' => 5]);
    21	
    22	        $rankedBooks = $this->get('/ranking')->assertOk()->viewData('rankedBooks');
    23	
    24	        $this->assertSame($high->id, $rankedBooks->first()->id);
    25	        $this->assertTrue(
    26	            $rankedBooks->search(fn ($b) => $b->id === $high->id) <
    27	            $rankedBooks->search(fn ($b) => $b->id === $low->id)
    28	        );
    29	    }
    30	
    31	    /** F-K2: レビュー0件は対象外 */
    32	    public function test_ranking_excludes_books_without_reviews(): void
    33	    {
    34	        $reviewed = Book::factory()->create(['title' => 'レビューあり本']);
    35	        $noReview = Book::factory()->create(['title' => 'レビューなし本']);
    36	        Review::factory()->create(['book_id' => $reviewed->id, 'rating' => 4]);
    37	
    38	        $ids = $this->get('/ranking')->viewData('rankedBooks')->pluck('id');
    39	
    40	        $this->assertTrue($ids->contains($reviewed->id));
    41	        $this->assertFalse($ids->contains($noReview->id));
    42	    }
    43	
    44	    /** F-K3: 削除済み書籍は集計対象外 */
    45	    public function test_ranking_excludes_deleted_books(): void
    46	    {
    47	        $book = Book::factory()->create(['title' => '削除される本']);
    48	        Review::factory()->create(['book_id' => $book->id, 'rating' => 5]);
    49	        $book->delete();
    50	
    51	        $this->get('/ranking')
    52	            ->assertOk()
    53	            ->assertDontSee('削除される本');
    54	    }
    55	
    56	    /** F-K4: 未ログインでも閲覧可 */
    57	    public function test_ranking_public(): void
    58	    {
    59	        $this->get('/ranking')->assertOk();
    60	    }
    61	}

===== tests/Feature/ReadingPlanReminderTest.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Tests\Feature;
     4	
     5	use App\Enums\NotificationTiming;
     6	use App\Enums\ReadingPlanStatus;
     7	use App\Models\Book;
     8	use App\Models\ReadingPlan;
     9	use App\Models\User;
    10	use Carbon\Carbon;
    11	use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
    12	use Tests\TestCase;
    13	
    14	/**
    15	 * 走行⑫：リマインダーバッチ reading-plans:send-reminders（検品表 K）。
    16	 * three_days_before・on_due_date は in_progress のみ。
    17	 * three_days_after は in_progress ＋ expired（完了以外）を対象とする。
    18	 */
    19	class ReadingPlanReminderTest extends TestCase
    20	{
    21	    use RefreshDatabase;
    22	
    23	    protected function setUp(): void
    24	    {
    25	        parent::setUp();
    26	        Carbon::setTestNow(Carbon::parse('2026-09-17'));
    27	    }
    28	
    29	    protected function tearDown(): void
    30	    {
    31	        Carbon::setTestNow(null);
    32	        parent::tearDown();
    33	    }
    34	
    35	    /**
    36	     * 指定ユーザー・書籍・状態・期日の計画を作る。
    37	     */
    38	    private function makePlan(User $user, ReadingPlanStatus $status, string $targetDate): ReadingPlan
    39	    {
    40	        return ReadingPlan::create([
    41	            'user_id' => $user->id,
    42	            'book_id' => Book::factory()->create()->id,
    43	            'target_date' => $targetDate,
    44	            'status' => $status,
    45	        ]);
    46	    }
    47	
    48	    /**
    49	     * 指定ユーザーが受け取った通知の timing 値の一覧。
    50	     *
    51	     * @return array<int, string>
    52	     */
    53	    private function timingsOf(User $user): array
    54	    {
    55	        return $user->fresh()->notifications
    56	            ->map(fn ($n) => $n->data['timing'])
    57	            ->all();
    58	    }
    59	
    60	    /** K-1: three_days_before・on_due_date は in_progress のみを対象とする */
    61	    public function test_three_days_before_and_on_due_date_target_in_progress_only(): void
    62	    {
    63	        // 3日前リマインダー対象（in_progress・target=today+3）
    64	        $beforeUser = User::factory()->create();
    65	        $this->makePlan($beforeUser, ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress, Carbon::today()->addDays(3)->toDateString());
    66	
    67	        // 当日リマインダー対象（in_progress・target=today）
    68	        $dueUser = User::factory()->create();
    69	        $this->makePlan($dueUser, ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress, Carbon::today()->toDateString());
    70	
    71	        // completedは対象外（target=today+3, today でそれぞれ）
    72	        $completedBeforeUser = User::factory()->create();
    73	        $this->makePlan($completedBeforeUser, ReadingPlanStatus::Completed, Carbon::today()->addDays(3)->toDateString());
    74	        $completedDueUser = User::factory()->create();
    75	        $this->makePlan($completedDueUser, ReadingPlanStatus::Completed, Carbon::today()->toDateString());
    76	
    77	        $this->artisan('reading-plans:send-reminders')->assertExitCode(0);
    78	
    79	        $this->assertSame([NotificationTiming::ThreeDaysBefore->value], $this->timingsOf($beforeUser));
    80	        $this->assertSame([NotificationTiming::OnDueDate->value], $this->timingsOf($dueUser));
    81	        $this->assertSame([], $this->timingsOf($completedBeforeUser));
    82	        $this->assertSame([], $this->timingsOf($completedDueUser));
    83	    }
    84	
    85	    /** K-2: three_days_after は in_progress ＋ expired を対象とし、expired にも通知される */
    86	    public function test_three_days_after_targets_in_progress_and_expired(): void
    87	    {
    88	        // expired・target=today-3 → three_days_after 通知される
    89	        $expiredUser = User::factory()->create();
    90	        $this->makePlan($expiredUser, ReadingPlanStatus::Expired, Carbon::today()->subDays(3)->toDateString());
    91	
    92	        // in_progress・target=today-3 → three_days_after 通知される
    93	        $inProgressUser = User::factory()->create();
    94	        $this->makePlan($inProgressUser, ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress, Carbon::today()->subDays(3)->toDateString());
    95	
    96	        // completed・target=today-3 → 対象外
    97	        $completedUser = User::factory()->create();
    98	        $this->makePlan($completedUser, ReadingPlanStatus::Completed, Carbon::today()->subDays(3)->toDateString());
    99	
   100	        $this->artisan('reading-plans:send-reminders')->assertExitCode(0);
   101	
   102	        $this->assertSame([NotificationTiming::ThreeDaysAfter->value], $this->timingsOf($expiredUser));
   103	        $this->assertSame([NotificationTiming::ThreeDaysAfter->value], $this->timingsOf($inProgressUser));
   104	        $this->assertSame([], $this->timingsOf($completedUser));
   105	    }
   106	
   107	    /**
   108	     * K-3: シードシナリオ（計画#3の二重シナリオ）。
   109	     * target=today-3・in_progress の計画は 0:00 失効バッチで expired 化し、
   110	     * 7:00 リマインダーで three_days_after 通知が作成される。
   111	     */
   112	    public function test_plan3_double_scenario(): void
   113	    {
   114	        $user = User::factory()->create();
   115	        $plan = $this->makePlan($user, ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress, Carbon::today()->subDays(3)->toDateString());
   116	
   117	        // 0:00 失効バッチ → expired 化
   118	        $this->artisan('reading-plans:expire')->assertExitCode(0);
   119	        $this->assertSame(ReadingPlanStatus::Expired, $plan->fresh()->status);
   120	
   121	        // 7:00 リマインダーバッチ → three_days_after 通知
   122	        $this->artisan('reading-plans:send-reminders')->assertExitCode(0);
   123	
   124	        $this->assertSame([NotificationTiming::ThreeDaysAfter->value], $this->timingsOf($user));
   125	        $this->assertDatabaseHas('notifications', [
   126	            'notifiable_id' => $user->id,
   127	        ]);
   128	    }
   129	}

===== tests/Feature/ReadingPlanTest.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Tests\Feature;
     4	
     5	use App\Enums\ReadingPlanStatus;
     6	use App\Models\Book;
     7	use App\Models\ReadingPlan;
     8	use App\Models\User;
     9	use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
    10	use Tests\TestCase;
    11	
    12	/**
    13	 * 走行⑪：読書計画CRUD・重複制御・編集制限（検品表 I）。
    14	 */
    15	class ReadingPlanTest extends TestCase
    16	{
    17	    use RefreshDatabase;
    18	
    19	    /**
    20	     * 読書計画レコードを直接作成するヘルパ（バリデーションを経由しない）。
    21	     */
    22	    private function makePlan(User $user, Book $book, ReadingPlanStatus $status, string $targetDate, ?string $completedAt = null): ReadingPlan
    23	    {
    24	        return ReadingPlan::create([
    25	            'user_id' => $user->id,
    26	            'book_id' => $book->id,
    27	            'target_date' => $targetDate,
    28	            'status' => $status,
    29	            'completed_at' => $completedAt,
    30	        ]);
    31	    }
    32	
    33	    // ================= I-1: CRUDの正常系（状態＋遷移先） =================
    34	
    35	    /** I-1: 作成の正常系（in_progressで作成され、一覧へリダイレクト） */
    36	    public function test_store_success(): void
    37	    {
    38	        $user = User::factory()->create();
    39	        $book = Book::factory()->create();
    40	
    41	        $response = $this->actingAs($user)->post('/reading-plans', [
    42	            'book_id' => $book->id,
    43	            'target_date' => now()->addDays(5)->toDateString(),
    44	        ]);
    45	
    46	        $response->assertRedirect(route('reading-plans.index'));
    47	        $response->assertSessionHas('success', '読書計画を登録しました');
    48	        $this->assertDatabaseHas('reading_plans', [
    49	            'user_id' => $user->id,
    50	            'book_id' => $book->id,
    51	            'status' => ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress->value,
    52	        ]);
    53	    }

   156	    /** I-4: 編集で target_date のみ変更でき、book_id/status を混ぜても変わらない */
   157	    public function test_update_changes_only_target_date(): void
   158	    {
   159	        $user = User::factory()->create();
   160	        $book = Book::factory()->create();
   161	        $otherBook = Book::factory()->create();
   162	        $plan = $this->makePlan($user, $book, ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress, now()->addDays(3)->toDateString());
   163	        $newDate = now()->addDays(9)->toDateString();
   164	
   165	        $this->actingAs($user)->put("/reading-plans/{$plan->id}", [
   166	            'target_date' => $newDate,
   167	            'book_id' => $otherBook->id,      // 混入させても無視される
   168	            'status' => ReadingPlanStatus::Completed->value, // 混入させても無視される
   169	        ])->assertRedirect(route('reading-plans.index'));
   170	
   171	        $fresh = $plan->fresh();
   172	        $this->assertSame($newDate, $fresh->target_date->toDateString());
   173	        $this->assertSame($book->id, $fresh->book_id);              // 変わらない
   174	        $this->assertSame(ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress, $fresh->status); // 変わらない
   175	    }

   263	    {

   266	        $completed = $this->makePlan($user, Book::factory()->create(['title' => '完了の本']), ReadingPlanStatus::Completed, now()->subDays(1)->toDateString(), now()->toDateTimeString());
   267	        // 他人の計画は表示されない。
   268	        $this->makePlan(User::factory()->create(), Book::factory()->create(['title' => '他人の本']), ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress, now()->addDays(3)->toDateString());

   274	    }

   288	    }

===== tests/Feature/ReadingReportTest.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Tests\Feature;
     4	
     5	use App\Models\Book;
     6	use App\Models\Genre;
     7	use App\Models\Review;
     8	use App\Models\User;
     9	use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
    10	use Tests\TestCase;
    11	
    12	/**
    13	 * 走行⑨：マイ読書レポート（検品表 F）。
    14	 * レビュー件数の固定値には依存せず、投入したレビューの内容から統計を検証する。
    15	 */
    16	class ReadingReportTest extends TestCase
    17	{
    18	    use RefreshDatabase;
    19	
    20	    /** F-1 / F-2: 4種の統計（総レビュー数・読了冊数・評価分布・高評価TOP5・ジャンル別TOP5）を確認する */
    21	    public function test_report_shows_all_statistics(): void
    22	    {
    23	        $user = User::factory()->create();
    24	        $genreTech = Genre::factory()->create(['name' => '技術書']);
    25	        $genreNovel = Genre::factory()->create(['name' => '小説']);
    26	
    27	        $bookA = Book::factory()->create(['title' => '高評価の本', 'author' => '著者A', 'user_id' => $user->id]);
    28	        $bookA->genres()->sync([$genreTech->id]);
    29	        $bookB = Book::factory()->create(['title' => '低評価の本', 'author' => '著者B', 'user_id' => $user->id]);
    30	        $bookB->genres()->sync([$genreNovel->id]);
    31	
    32	        // 自分のレビュー：bookA=5, bookB=2。他人のレビューは集計対象外。
    33	        Review::factory()->create(['user_id' => $user->id, 'book_id' => $bookA->id, 'rating' => 5]);
    34	        Review::factory()->create(['user_id' => $user->id, 'book_id' => $bookB->id, 'rating' => 2]);
    35	        Review::factory()->create(['user_id' => User::factory()->create()->id, 'book_id' => $bookA->id, 'rating' => 1]);
    36	
    37	        $response = $this->actingAs($user)->get('/reports');
    38	        $response->assertOk();
    39	
    40	        $stats = $response->viewData('stats');
    41	
    42	        // 基本サマリー：総レビュー数=2、読了冊数（ユニーク書籍数）=2、平均=(5+2)/2=3.5
    43	        $this->assertSame(2, $stats['summary']['total_reviews']);
    44	        $this->assertSame(2, $stats['summary']['books_read']);
    45	        $this->assertSame(3.5, $stats['summary']['average_rating']);
    46	
    47	        // 評価分布：添字0=★1〜添字4=★5。★2が1件、★5が1件。
    48	        $this->assertSame(1, $stats['rating_distribution'][1]); // ★2
    49	        $this->assertSame(1, $stats['rating_distribution'][4]); // ★5
    50	        $this->assertSame(0, $stats['rating_distribution'][0]); // ★1（他人分は含めない）
    51	
    52	        // 高評価TOP5：4以上のみ。bookAのみ該当。
    53	        $topIds = collect($stats['top_rated_books'])->pluck('id')->all();
    54	        $this->assertContains($bookA->id, $topIds);
    55	        $this->assertNotContains($bookB->id, $topIds);
    56	
    57	        // ジャンル別TOP5：技術書=5.0、小説=2.0。
    58	        $genreRatings = collect($stats['genre_ratings'])->keyBy('name');
    59	        $this->assertSame(5.0, (float) $genreRatings['技術書']['average_rating']);
    60	        $this->assertSame(2.0, (float) $genreRatings['小説']['average_rating']);
    61	    }
    62	
    63	    /**
    64	     * F-2: 統計がレビュー件数の固定値に依存していないことの明示。
    65	     * 同一書籍への自分の複数レビュー（可変件数）でも読了冊数はユニーク書籍数で数える。
    66	     */
    67	    public function test_books_read_counts_unique_books_not_review_count(): void
    68	    {
    69	        $user = User::factory()->create();
    70	        $book = Book::factory()->create(['user_id' => $user->id]);
    71	        $book->genres()->sync([Genre::factory()->create()->id]);
    72	
    73	        // 同じ本に3件のレビュー（rand(2,4)相当の可変件数を模す）。
    74	        Review::factory()->count(3)->create(['user_id' => $user->id, 'book_id' => $book->id, 'rating' => 4]);
    75	
    76	        $stats = $this->actingAs($user)->get('/reports')->viewData('stats');
    77	
    78	        $this->assertSame(3, $stats['summary']['total_reviews']);
    79	        $this->assertSame(1, $stats['summary']['books_read']); // 件数3でも冊数は1
    80	    }
    81	
    82	    /** F-3: 論理削除済み書籍への自分のレビューも集計・リンクに含まれる */
    83	    public function test_soft_deleted_book_review_included_in_report(): void
    84	    {
    85	        $user = User::factory()->create();
    86	        $genre = Genre::factory()->create(['name' => 'ジャンルX']);
    87	        $book = Book::factory()->create(['title' => '削除済みだが高評価', 'user_id' => $user->id]);
    88	        $book->genres()->sync([$genre->id]);
    89	
    90	        Review::factory()->create(['user_id' => $user->id, 'book_id' => $book->id, 'rating' => 5]);
    91	
    92	        $book->delete();
    93	
    94	        $response = $this->actingAs($user)->get('/reports');
    95	        $response->assertOk();
    96	        $stats = $response->viewData('stats');
    97	
    98	        // 削除済み書籍のレビューも総レビュー数・高評価TOP5・ジャンル別に含まれる。
    99	        $this->assertSame(1, $stats['summary']['total_reviews']);
   100	        $this->assertContains($book->id, collect($stats['top_rated_books'])->pluck('id')->all());
   101	        $this->assertContains('ジャンルX', collect($stats['genre_ratings'])->pluck('name')->all());
   102	        // 一覧のリンク（削除済み書籍の詳細）が表示されている。
   103	        $response->assertSee('削除済みだが高評価');
   104	    }
   105	
   106	    /** 認可：レポートは未ログインだと/loginへリダイレクト */
   107	    public function test_report_requires_authentication(): void
   108	    {
   109	        $this->get('/reports')->assertRedirect('/login');
   110	    }
   111	}

===== tests/Feature/ReviewLikeTest.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Tests\Feature;
     4	
     5	use App\Models\Book;
     6	use App\Models\Review;
     7	use App\Models\User;
     8	use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
     9	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
    10	use Tests\TestCase;
    11	
    12	class ReviewLikeTest extends TestCase
    13	{
    14	    use RefreshDatabase;
    15	
    16	    /** F-L1: いいねトグルON/OFF */
    17	    public function test_like_toggle_on_off(): void
    18	    {
    19	        $user = User::factory()->create();
    20	        $review = Review::factory()->create();
    21	
    22	        $this->actingAs($user)->post(route('reviews.like', $review));
    23	        $this->assertDatabaseHas('review_likes', ['review_id' => $review->id, 'user_id' => $user->id]);
    24	
    25	        $this->actingAs($user)->post(route('reviews.like', $review));
    26	        $this->assertDatabaseMissing('review_likes', ['review_id' => $review->id, 'user_id' => $user->id]);
    27	    }
    28	
    29	    /** F-L2: 削除済み書籍下でのいいね */
    30	    public function test_like_on_deleted_book_review(): void
    31	    {

    43	            ->assertSessionHas('success');

    45	    }
    46

    51	        $book = Book::factory()->create();
    52	
    53	        $this->actingAs($user)->post(route('reviews.store', $book), ['comment' => 'x'])
    54	            ->assertSessionHasErrors('rating');
    55	    }
    56	
    57	    /** F-R2: 編集の認可 */
    58	    public function test_edit_authorization(): void
    59	    {
    60	        $owner = User::factory()->create();
    61	        $other = User::factory()->create();
    62	        $review = Review::factory()->create(['user_id' => $owner->id]);
    63	
    64	        $this->actingAs($owner)->get(route('reviews.edit', $review))->assertOk();
    65	        $this->actingAs($other)->get(route('reviews.edit', $review))->assertForbidden();
    66	
    67	        $this->actingAs($other)->put(route('reviews.update', $review), ['rating' => 5])
    68	            ->assertForbidden();
    69	        $this->actingAs($owner)->put(route('reviews.update', $review), ['rating' => 5, 'comment' => '更新'])
    70	            ->assertRedirect(route('books.show', $review->book));
    71	        $this->assertDatabaseHas('reviews', ['id' => $review->id, 'rating' => 5, 'comment' => '更新']);
    72	    }
    73	
    74	    /** F-R3: 削除の認可 */
    75	    public function test_delete_authorization(): void
    76	    {
    77	        $owner = User::factory()->create();
    78	        $other = User::factory()->create();
    79	        $review = Review::factory()->create(['user_id' => $owner->id]);
    80	
    81	        $this->actingAs($other)->delete(route('reviews.destroy', $review))->assertForbidden();
    82	        $this->assertDatabaseHas('reviews', ['id' => $review->id]);
    83	
    84	        $this->actingAs($owner)->delete(route('reviews.destroy', $review))
    85	            ->assertRedirect(route('books.show', $review->book))
    86	            ->assertSessionHas('success', 'レビューを削除しました');
    87	        $this->assertDatabaseMissing('reviews', ['id' => $review->id]);
    88	    }
    89	
    90	    /** F-R4: いいねのcascade */
    91	    public function test_review_delete_cascades_likes(): void
    92	    {
    93	        $owner = User::factory()->create();
    94	        $liker = User::factory()->create();
    95	        $review = Review::factory()->create(['user_id' => $owner->id]);
    96	        $review->likedByUsers()->attach($liker->id);
    97	
    98	        $this->assertSame(1, \DB::table('review_likes')->where('review_id', $review->id)->count());
    99	
   100	        $this->actingAs($owner)->delete(route('reviews.destroy', $review));
   101	
   102	        $this->assertSame(0, \DB::table('review_likes')->where('review_id', $review->id)->count());
   103	    }
   104	
   105	    /** F-R5: 削除済み書籍下でのレビュー編集・削除 */
   106	    public function test_review_operations_on_deleted_book(): void
   107	    {
   108	        $owner = User::factory()->create();
   109	        $book = Book::factory()->create();
   110	        $review = Review::factory()->create(['user_id' => $owner->id, 'book_id' => $book->id]);
   111	        $book->delete();
   112	
   113	        // 編集画面到達可能
   114	        $this->actingAs($owner)->get(route('reviews.edit', $review))->assertOk();
   115	
   116	        // 更新可能
   117	        $this->actingAs($owner)->put(route('reviews.update', $review), ['rating' => 2, 'comment' => 'z'])
   118	            ->assertRedirect(route('books.show', $book));
   119	
   120	        // 削除可能
   121	        $this->actingAs($owner)->delete(route('reviews.destroy', $review))
   122	            ->assertSessionHas('success');
   123	        $this->assertDatabaseMissing('reviews', ['id' => $review->id]);
   124	    }
   125	}

===== tests/Feature/ScheduleTest.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Tests\Feature;
     4	
     5	use Illuminate\Console\Scheduling\Schedule;
     6	use Tests\TestCase;
     7	
     8	/**
     9	 * 走行⑪⑫：バッチのスケジュール登録（検品表⑭差し戻し 3）。
    10	 * reading-plans:expire=0:00、reading-plans:send-reminders=7:00 の登録を確認する。
    11	 */
    12	class ScheduleTest extends TestCase
    13	{
    14	    /**
    15	     * 登録済みイベントから、コマンド名を含むイベントのcron式を引く。
    16	     */
    17	    private function expressionForCommand(string $needle): ?string
    18	    {
    19	        $schedule = $this->app->make(Schedule::class);
    20	
    21	        foreach ($schedule->events() as $event) {
    22	            if (str_contains($event->command ?? '', $needle)) {
    23	                return $event->expression;
    24	            }
    25	        }
    26	
    27	        return null;
    28	    }
    29	
    30	    /** reading-plans:expire が毎日0:00に登録されている */
    31	    public function test_expire_scheduled_at_midnight(): void
    32	    {
    33	        $this->assertSame('0 0 * * *', $this->expressionForCommand('reading-plans:expire'));
    34	    }
    35	
    36	    /** reading-plans:send-reminders が毎日7:00に登録されている */
    37	    public function test_reminders_scheduled_at_seven(): void
    38	    {
    39	        $this->assertSame('0 7 * * *', $this->expressionForCommand('reading-plans:send-reminders'));
    40	    }
    41	}

===== tests/Feature/SeederTest.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Tests\Feature;
     4	
     5	use Database\Seeders\DatabaseSeeder;
     6	use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
     7	use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
     8	use Tests\TestCase;
     9	
    10	class SeederTest extends TestCase
    11	{
    12	    use RefreshDatabase;
    13	
    14	    /** F-P11: db:seed後の件数（reviewsは各書籍rand(2,4)のため固定値では確認しない） */
    15	    public function test_seed_counts(): void
    16	    {
    17	        $this->seed(DatabaseSeeder::class);
    18	
    19	        $this->assertSame(5, DB::table('users')->count());
    20	        $this->assertSame(10, DB::table('genres')->count());
    21	        $this->assertSame(11, DB::table('books')->count());
    22	
    23	        // 各書籍のレビューは2〜4件の範囲に収まる（件数固定を前提にしない・発注書§2-3）。
    24	        $perBook = DB::table('reviews')
    25	            ->select('book_id', DB::raw('COUNT(*) as c'))
    26	            ->groupBy('book_id')
    27	            ->pluck('c', 'book_id');
    28	
    29	        $this->assertCount(11, $perBook); // 全書籍にレビューが1件以上付く
    30	        foreach ($perBook as $count) {
    31	            $this->assertGreaterThanOrEqual(2, $count);
    32	            $this->assertLessThanOrEqual(4, $count);
    33	        }
    34	
    35	        // 総数は 11冊 × 2〜4件 = 22〜44 の範囲に収まる。
    36	        $total = DB::table('reviews')->count();
    37	        $this->assertGreaterThanOrEqual(22, $total);
    38	        $this->assertLessThanOrEqual(44, $total);
    39	    }
    40	
    41	    /** F-P12: 2回実行しても重複しない（firstOrCreate） */
    42	    public function test_seed_is_idempotent(): void
    43	    {
    44	        $this->seed(DatabaseSeeder::class);
    45	        $this->seed(DatabaseSeeder::class);
    46	
    47	        $this->assertSame(5, DB::table('users')->count());
    48	        $this->assertSame(10, DB::table('genres')->count());
    49	        $this->assertSame(11, DB::table('books')->count());
    50	    }
    51	}

===== tests/Feature/WebErrorPageTest.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Tests\Feature;
     4	
     5	use App\Models\Book;
     6	use App\Models\Genre;
     7	use App\Models\User;
     8	use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;

    14	/**
    15	 * 走行⑮：Web画面エラーの日本語化とAPI側のJSON非破壊（検品表 L）。

    18	{
    19	    use RefreshDatabase;
    20	
    21	    /** L-1: 存在しないWeb URLは日本語の404ページを返す */

    23	    {

    25	
    26	        $response->assertStatus(404)
    27	            ->assertSee('ページが見つかりません')

    29	    }
    30	
    31	    /** L-1: 権限のないWeb操作は日本語の403ページを返す */

    35	        $other = User::factory()->create();

    41	            ->assertSee('このページにアクセスする権限がありません');
    42	    }
    43

    48	            throw new TokenMismatchException('CSRF token mismatch.');
    49	        });
    50	
    51	        $response = $this->get('/__test_token_mismatch');
    52	
    53	        $response->assertStatus(419)
    54	            ->assertSee('セッションの有効期限が切れました');
    55	    }
    56	
    57	    /** L-2: API向けの404はJSON応答（非破壊）のまま */
    58	    public function test_api_404_returns_json(): void
    59	    {
    60	        $response = $this->getJson('/api/v1/books/99999');
    61	
    62	        $response->assertStatus(404)
    63	            ->assertHeader('content-type', 'application/json')
    64	            ->assertExactJson(['message' => '指定された書籍が見つかりません。']);
    65	    }
    66	
    67	    /** L-2: API向けの401はJSON応答（非破壊）のまま */
    68	    public function test_api_401_returns_json(): void
    69	    {
    70	        $response = $this->postJson('/api/v1/books', []);
    71	
    72	        $response->assertStatus(401)
    73	            ->assertHeader('content-type', 'application/json')
    74	            ->assertExactJson(['message' => '認証が必要です。']);
    75	    }
    76	
    77	    /** L-2: API向けの403はJSON応答（非破壊）のまま */
    78	    public function test_api_403_returns_json(): void
    79	    {
    80	        $owner = User::factory()->create();
    81	        $other = User::factory()->create();
    82	        $book = Book::factory()->create(['user_id' => $owner->id]);
    83	        $book->genres()->sync([Genre::factory()->create()->id]);
    84	        Sanctum::actingAs($other);
    85	
    86	        $response = $this->putJson("/api/v1/books/{$book->id}", [
    87	            'title' => 'x',
    88	            'author' => 'y',
    89	            'isbn' => '9784999999999',
    90	            'published_date' => '2020-01-01',
    91	            'genres' => [Genre::factory()->create()->id],
    92	        ]);
    93	
    94	        $response->assertStatus(403)
    95	            ->assertHeader('content-type', 'application/json')
    96	            ->assertExactJson(['message' => 'この操作を実行する権限がありません。']);
    97	    }
    98	}

===== tests/Unit/BookTest.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Tests\Unit;
     4	
     5	use App\Models\Book;
     6	use App\Models\Genre;
     7	use App\Models\Review;
     8	use App\Models\User;
     9	use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
    10	use Tests\TestCase;
    11	
    12	class BookTest extends TestCase
    13	{
    14	    use RefreshDatabase;
    15	
    16	    /** U-B1: Book belongsTo User */
    17	    public function test_book_belongs_to_user(): void
    18	    {
    19	        $user = User::factory()->create();
    20	        $book = Book::factory()->create(['user_id' => $user->id]);
    21	
    22	        $this->assertInstanceOf(User::class, $book->user);
    23	        $this->assertSame($user->id, $book->user->id);
    24	    }
    25	
    26	    /** U-B2: Book belongsToMany Genre (book_genre) */
    27	    public function test_book_belongs_to_many_genres(): void
    28	    {
    29	        $book = Book::factory()->create();
    30	        $genres = Genre::factory()->count(2)->create();
    31	        $book->genres()->sync($genres->pluck('id')->all());
    32	
    33	        $this->assertCount(2, $book->fresh()->genres);
    34	        $this->assertInstanceOf(Genre::class, $book->genres->first());
    35	    }
    36	
    37	    /** U-B3: Book hasMany Review */
    38	    public function test_book_has_many_reviews(): void
    39	    {
    40	        $book = Book::factory()->create();
    41	        Review::factory()->count(3)->create(['book_id' => $book->id]);
    42	
    43	        $this->assertCount(3, $book->fresh()->reviews);
    44	        $this->assertInstanceOf(Review::class, $book->reviews->first());
    45	    }
    46	
    47	    /** U-B4: withAvg reviews rating / null when no reviews */
    48	    public function test_reviews_avg_rating(): void
    49	    {
    50	        $book = Book::factory()->create();
    51	        Review::factory()->create(['book_id' => $book->id, 'rating' => 4]);
    52	        Review::factory()->create(['book_id' => $book->id, 'rating' => 2]);
    53	
    54	        $withReviews = Book::withAvg('reviews', 'rating')->find($book->id);
    55	        $this->assertEquals(3.0, (float) $withReviews->reviews_avg_rating);
    56	
    57	        $noReview = Book::factory()->create();
    58	        $result = Book::withAvg('reviews', 'rating')->find($noReview->id);
    59	        $this->assertNull($result->reviews_avg_rating);
    60	    }
    61	
    62	    /** U-B5: SoftDeletes standard exclusion */
    63	    public function test_soft_delete_exclusion(): void
    64	    {
    65	        $book = Book::factory()->create();
    66	        $book->delete();
    67	
    68	        $this->assertNull(Book::find($book->id));
    69	        $this->assertNotNull(Book::withTrashed()->find($book->id));
    70	    }
    71	}

===== tests/Unit/ExpireReadingPlansBoundaryTest.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Tests\Unit;
     4	
     5	use App\Enums\ReadingPlanStatus;
     6	use App\Models\Book;
     7	use App\Models\ReadingPlan;
     8	use App\Models\User;
     9	use Carbon\Carbon;
    10	use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
    11	use Tests\TestCase;
    12	
    13	/**
    14	 * 走行⑪：自動失効バッチの日付境界判定の補強（検品表 J、発注書§5）。
    15	 * 3日前ちょうど（=対象）と4日前（=非対象）の等値境界を単体で突く。
    16	 */
    17	class ExpireReadingPlansBoundaryTest extends TestCase
    18	{
    19	    use RefreshDatabase;
    20	
    21	    protected function setUp(): void
    22	    {
    23	        parent::setUp();
    24	        Carbon::setTestNow(Carbon::parse('2026-09-17'));
    25	    }
    26	
    27	    protected function tearDown(): void
    28	    {
    29	        Carbon::setTestNow(null);
    30	        parent::tearDown();
    31	    }
    32	
    33	    private function makeInProgress(string $targetDate): ReadingPlan
    34	    {
    35	        return ReadingPlan::create([
    36	            'user_id' => User::factory()->create()->id,
    37	            'book_id' => Book::factory()->create()->id,
    38	            'target_date' => $targetDate,
    39	            'status' => ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress,
    40	        ]);
    41	    }
    42	
    43	    /**
    44	     * 境界：3日前ちょうどは失効、4日前は据え置き（等値比較であることの確認）。
    45	     *
    46	     * @return array<string, array{0: string, 1: ReadingPlanStatus}>
    47	     */
    48	    public static function boundaryProvider(): array
    49	    {
    50	        return [
    51	            '3日前ちょうど→失効' => ['-3 days', ReadingPlanStatus::Expired],
    52	            '4日前→据え置き' => ['-4 days', ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress],
    53	            '2日前→据え置き' => ['-2 days', ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress],
    54	        ];
    55	    }
    56	
    57	    /**
    58	     * @dataProvider boundaryProvider
    59	     */
    60	    public function test_boundary(string $modifier, ReadingPlanStatus $expected): void
    61	    {
    62	        $plan = $this->makeInProgress(Carbon::today()->modify($modifier)->toDateString());
    63	
    64	        $this->artisan('reading-plans:expire')->assertExitCode(0);
    65	
    66	        $this->assertSame($expected, $plan->fresh()->status);
    67	    }
    68	}

    11	{
    12	    use RefreshDatabase;
    13	
    14	    /** U-F1: favoriteBooks belongsToMany toggle works */
    15	    public function test_favorite_books_toggle(): void
    16	    {
    17	        $user = User::factory()->create();
    18	        $book = Book::factory()->create();
    19	
    20	        $user->favoriteBooks()->toggle($book->id);
    21	        $this->assertTrue($user->fresh()->favoriteBooks->contains($book->id));
    22	
    23	        $user->favoriteBooks()->toggle($book->id);
    24	        $this->assertFalse($user->fresh()->favoriteBooks->contains($book->id));
    25	    }
    26	}

===== tests/Unit/ModelRelationTest.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Tests\Unit;
     4	
     5	use App\Enums\ReadingPlanStatus;
     6	use App\Models\Book;
     7	use App\Models\ReadingPlan;
     8	use App\Models\Review;
     9	use App\Models\User;
    10	use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
    11	use Tests\TestCase;
    12	
    13	/**
    14	 * 応用フェーズで追加されたモデルの未テストリレーションを確認する（検品表⑭差し戻し 5）。
    15	 * Book::favoritedByUsers / User::books・reviews・readingPlans。
    16	 */
    17	class ModelRelationTest extends TestCase
    18	{
    19	    use RefreshDatabase;
    20	
    21	    /** Book::favoritedByUsers() が favorites 中間テーブル経由でユーザーを返す */
    22	    public function test_book_favorited_by_users(): void
    23	    {
    24	        $book = Book::factory()->create();
    25	        $user = User::factory()->create();
    26	        $user->favoriteBooks()->attach($book->id);
    27	
    28	        $this->assertTrue($book->favoritedByUsers->contains($user));
    29	        $this->assertSame(1, $book->favoritedByUsers()->count());
    30	    }
    31	
    32	    /** User::books() が自分の登録書籍を返す */
    33	    public function test_user_books(): void
    34	    {
    35	        $user = User::factory()->create();
    36	        Book::factory()->count(2)->create(['user_id' => $user->id]);
    37	        Book::factory()->create(['user_id' => User::factory()->create()->id]);
    38	
    39	        $this->assertSame(2, $user->books()->count());
    40	        $this->assertTrue($user->books->every(fn (Book $b) => $b->user_id === $user->id));
    41	    }
    42	
    43	    /** User::reviews() が自分のレビューを返す */
    44	    public function test_user_reviews(): void
    45	    {
    46	        $user = User::factory()->create();
    47	        $book = Book::factory()->create();
    48	        Review::factory()->count(3)->create(['user_id' => $user->id, 'book_id' => $book->id]);
    49	        Review::factory()->create(['user_id' => User::factory()->create()->id, 'book_id' => $book->id]);
    50	
    51	        $this->assertSame(3, $user->reviews()->count());
    52	    }
    53	
    54	    /** User::readingPlans() が自分の読書計画を返す */
    55	    public function test_user_reading_plans(): void
    56	    {
    57	        $user = User::factory()->create();
    58	        ReadingPlan::create([
    59	            'user_id' => $user->id,
    60	            'book_id' => Book::factory()->create()->id,
    61	            'target_date' => now()->addDays(3)->toDateString(),
    62	            'status' => ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress,
    63	        ]);
    64	
    65	        $this->assertSame(1, $user->readingPlans()->count());
    66	        $this->assertSame($user->id, $user->readingPlans->first()->user_id);
    67	    }
    68	}

===== tests/Unit/ReadingPlanStatusTest.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Tests\Unit;
     4	
     5	use App\Enums\ReadingPlanStatus;
     6	use PHPUnit\Framework\TestCase;
     7	
     8	/**
     9	 * 走行⑪：ReadingPlanStatus Enum の表示ヘルパ（検品表⑭差し戻し 2）。
    10	 * label()・badgeClass() が各caseで確定文言・確定クラスを返すことを確認する。
    11	 */
    12	class ReadingPlanStatusTest extends TestCase
    13	{
    14	    /** label() が各caseで正しい日本語ラベルを返す */
    15	    public function test_label_for_each_case(): void
    16	    {
    17	        $this->assertSame('進行中', ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress->label());
    18	        $this->assertSame('完了', ReadingPlanStatus::Completed->label());
    19	        $this->assertSame('期限切れ', ReadingPlanStatus::Expired->label());
    20	    }
    21	
    22	    /** badgeClass() が各caseで正しいTailwindクラス文字列を返す */
    23	    public function test_badge_class_for_each_case(): void
    24	    {
    25	        $this->assertSame('bg-blue-100 text-blue-800', ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress->badgeClass());
    26	        $this->assertSame('bg-green-100 text-green-800', ReadingPlanStatus::Completed->badgeClass());
    27	        $this->assertSame('bg-red-100 text-red-800', ReadingPlanStatus::Expired->badgeClass());
    28	    }
    29	
    30	    /** enumのvalue（DB格納値）が確定値である */
    31	    public function test_enum_values(): void
    32	    {
    33	        $this->assertSame('in_progress', ReadingPlanStatus::InProgress->value);
    34	        $this->assertSame('completed', ReadingPlanStatus::Completed->value);
    35	        $this->assertSame('expired', ReadingPlanStatus::Expired->value);
    36	    }
    37	}

===== tests/Unit/ReviewTest.php
     1	<?php
     2	
     3	namespace Tests\Unit;
     4	
     5	use App\Models\Book;
     6	use App\Models\Review;
     7	use App\Models\User;
     8	use Illuminate\Foundation\Testing\RefreshDatabase;
     9	use Tests\TestCase;
    10	
    11	class ReviewTest extends TestCase
    12	{
    13	    use RefreshDatabase;
    14	
    15	    /** U-R1: Review belongsTo Book */
    16	    public function test_review_belongs_to_book(): void
    17	    {
    18	        $book = Book::factory()->create();
    19	        $review = Review::factory()->create(['book_id' => $book->id]);
    20	
    21	        $this->assertInstanceOf(Book::class, $review->book);
    22	        $this->assertSame($book->id, $review->book->id);
    23	    }
    24	
    25	    /** U-R2: Review belongsTo User */
    26	    public function test_review_belongs_to_user(): void
    27	    {
    28	        $user = User::factory()->create();
    29	        $review = Review::factory()->create(['user_id' => $user->id]);
    30	
    31	        $this->assertInstanceOf(User::class, $review->user);
    32	        $this->assertSame($user->id, $review->user->id);
    33	    }
    34	
    35	    /** U-R3: Review belongsToMany User (likedByUsers) */
    36	    public function test_review_liked_by_users(): void
    37	    {
    38	        $review = Review::factory()->create();
    39	        $users = User::factory()->count(2)->create();
    40	        $review->likedByUsers()->sync($users->pluck('id')->all());
    41	
    42	        $this->assertCount(2, $review->fresh()->likedByUsers);
    43	        $this->assertInstanceOf(User::class, $review->likedByUsers->first());
    44	    }
    45	
    46	    /** U-R4: Review::book() has withTrashed */
    47	    public function test_review_book_uses_with_trashed(): void
    48	    {
    49	        $book = Book::factory()->create();
    50	        $review = Review::factory()->create(['book_id' => $book->id]);
    51	        $book->delete();
    52	
    53	        $this->assertNotNull($review->fresh()->book);
    54	        $this->assertSame($book->id, $review->fresh()->book->id);
    55	    }
    56	}
